### [183] [Modeling Stochastic Multi-Agent Interaction in Intraday Battery Energy Storage Dispatch with Market Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01178)
*Ruimeng Hu,Mike Ludkovski,Hezhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了电网级电池储能系统日内调度的随机博弈模型，分析了多运营商竞争下的纳什均衡，得到了半显式反馈控制与均衡价格表征，并研究了竞争效应、协调收益、市场力量及大规模渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电网级电池储能系统日益普及的背景下，多个独立BESS运营商在同一电力市场中竞争性调度储能资源，其充电行为共同影响电价。现有研究缺乏对这种竞争性日内调度场景的严格博弈论建模，特别是考虑随机因素和电价内生化的情形。

Method: 采用随机线性二次型微分博弈框架，将电价内生化建模为充电率总和的函数。通过求解Riccati方程组，得到异构和同构两种设定下纳什均衡反馈控制和均衡价格的半显式表征。进一步分析竞争效应、协调收益、市场力量及大数量渐近行为。

Result: 得到了有限参与者随机线性二次型微分博弈的纳什均衡半显式解；刻画了额外BESS进入市场的边际外部性、运营商协调收益与市场力量、混合型BESS的供给效应；分析了参与者数量趋于无穷时的渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该模型为量化分析分散式BESS部署对电网的影响以及由此导致的日间电价价差缩小提供了定量测试平台，揭示了竞争性调度在储能经济性中的关键作用。

Abstract: We develop a stochastic game-theoretic model for intraday dispatch of grid-scale battery energy storage systems (BESSs). We assume that each BESS operator competitively manages her state-of-charge to maximize energy arbitrage revenues, driven by the endogenized electricity price that depends on the sum of the charging rates. We characterize the Nash equilibrium of the resulting finite-player linear-quadratic differential game with a shared stochastic driver, obtaining semi-explicit representations of equilibrium feedback controls and equilibrium prices both in the general heterogeneous and the simplified homogeneous BESS setting, via a system of Riccati equations. We then analyze competitive effects, including the marginal externality of additional BESS entering the market, the benefit of coordination and the corresponding market power of large operators, and supply effects from hybrid-type BESSs. We further study the asymptotic regime as the number of agents grows large. Our model provides a quantitative testbed to study the impact of decentralized BESS deployment on the grid and the resulting reduction in daily price spreads.

</details>


### [184] [A Single-Loop Stochastic Gradient Algorithm for Minimax Optimization with Nonlinear Coupled Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01246)
*Qichao Cao,Shangzhi Zeng,Jin Zhang,Yuxuan Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出SPACO算法，一种用于求解随机非凸-凹极小极大优化问题的单循环随机梯度方法，该问题带有非线性凸耦合约束。算法基于惩罚平滑近似框架，结合二次罚函数与正则化，提供了理论收敛保证和非渐近复杂度界，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以有效处理带非线性凸耦合约束的随机非凸-凹极小极大优化问题，需要一种具有理论保证且计算高效的算法。

Method: 提出SPACO算法，基于惩罚平滑近似框架，将二次罚函数与正则化相结合，将原约束问题转化为连续可微的近似问题，并采用单循环随机梯度更新进行求解。

Result: 建立了平滑框架的收敛保证，给出了非渐近复杂度界，通过渐近分析刻画了迭代序列聚点的稳定性，合成数据和实际机器学习任务上的实验验证了方法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: SPACO是一种理论上可靠且实际高效的算法，为带耦合约束的非凸-凹极小极大优化问题提供了新的求解思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a single-loop stochastic gradient algorithm for solving stochastic nonconvex-concave minimax optimization with nonlinear convex coupled constraints (MCC). The proposed method, SPACO (Stochastic Penalty-based Algorithm for minimax optimization with COupled constraints), is built upon a penalty-based smooth approximation framework for MCC. This framework integrates a quadratic penalty scheme with regularization to yield a continuously differentiable approximation of the MCC problem. We provide theoretical convergence guarantees for this smoothing framework. Furthermore, we establish non-asymptotic complexity bounds and provide an asymptotic analysis characterizing the stationarity of accumulation points for the iterates generated by SPACO. Experimental results on synthetic examples and practical machine learning tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [185] [Unified Lyapunov Method for ISS of PDEs: A Tutorial on Constructing Generalized Lyapunov Functionals for Parabolic and Hyperbolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01344)
*Jun Zheng,Guchuan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文档综述了广义Lyapunov方法（GLM）用于分析偏微分方程（PDE）输入到状态稳定性（ISS）的理论框架，通过三类典型PDE实例展示了如何系统构造依赖于外部输入的广义Lyapunov泛函（GLF）以建立L^q空间中的ISS估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lyapunov方法和标准ISS-Lyapunov定理在处理复杂边界扰动（特别是Dirichlet型扰动）时存在局限性，需要更灵活高效的分析工具。

Method: 提出广义Lyapunov方法（GLM），其核心是构建显式依赖于外部输入的广义Lyapunov泛函（GLF），并针对三类PDE（N维非线性抛物方程、一阶非线性双曲方程、二阶线性波方程）给出逐步构建步骤。

Result: 成功为三类具有边界扰动的PDE建立了L^q空间（q∈[2,∞]）中的显式ISS估计，验证了GLM的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: GLM为分析带复杂边界扰动的PDE提供了更灵活高效的框架，未来工作方向包括GLF的系统性构造方法及在控制器设计中的应用。

Abstract: This tutorial provides an overview of the generalized Lyapunov method (GLM) for analyzing input-to-state stability (ISS) of partial differential equations (PDEs). We begin by revisiting the classical Lyapunov method and the standard ISS-Lyapunov theorem, highlighting their limitations when applied to systems with complex boundary disturbances. In contrast, the GLM, based on the concept of generalized Lyapunov functionals (GLFs) that explicitly depend on the external input, offers greater flexibility and efficiency, particularly for PDEs with Dirichlet-type disturbances. The main objective of this tutorial is to demonstrate how to systematically construct GLFs to establish ISS estimates in $L^q$ spaces with any $q\in[2,\infty]$ for different PDEs. Specifically, we consider three representative classes of PDEs: (i) an $N$-dimensional nonlinear parabolic equation with mixed nonlinear boundary disturbances, (ii) a first order nonlinear hyperbolic equation with boundary disturbances, and (iii) a second order linear hyperbolic equation, i.e., a wave equation, with boundary damping and disturbances. For each case, we provide step-by-step constructions of appropriate GLFs and derive explicit ISS estimates, illustrating the general applicability of the GLM. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including the systematic construction of GLFs for broader classes of PDEs and their applications in controller design.

</details>


### [186] [Optimal control problem for a nonlinear nonlocal evolution system describing an interacting ternary mixture with an evaporating component: 2D case with bulk evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01377)
*Arghya Kundu,Adrian Muntean*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个最优控制问题来指导有机太阳能电池中形态类别的选择，重点关注聚合物溶剂混合物中的相分离过程以及溶剂蒸发对形态形成的控制机制，证明了最优控制的存在性，分析了控制到状态映射的Frechet导数，并通过伴随系统推导了一阶必要最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机太阳能电池的性能高度依赖于其活性层的形态结构，如何通过控制相分离过程中的参数（如溶剂蒸发）来获得理想的形态类别是提升器件效率的关键。本文旨在从数学最优控制的角度为这一形态选择问题提供理论依据。

Method: 论文将形态类别选择建模为一个最优控制问题，以溶剂蒸发作为控制手段来调控相分离过程。通过变分方法和泛函分析工具，证明了最优控制的存在性；进一步分析了控制到状态映射的Frechet可微性，并利用伴随方法推导了最优解满足的一阶必要条件。

Result: 建立了最优控制存在性的理论证明；得到了控制到状态映射的Frechet导数表达式；推导出了与原始系统耦合的伴随系统及相应的一阶最优性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为有机太阳能电池形态设计的参数优化提供了严格的数学框架，使得通过调节溶剂蒸发等工艺参数来定向控制活性层微观结构成为可能，有望指导实验中的工艺参数选择。

Abstract: We present an optimal control problem to guide the selection of morphology classes arising in organic solar cells. The study focuses on phase separation processes in polymer solvent mixtures, with particular attention to solvent evaporation as a mechanism to arrest morphology formation. We establish the existence of optimal controls and analyze the Frechet derivative of the control to state mapping. Finally, we derive the first order necessary optimality condition via the corresponding adjoint system.

</details>


### [187] [Quaternion Nonlinear Transform-Induced Nuclear Norm for Low-Rank Tensor Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01467)
*Biswarup Karmakar,Ratikanta Behera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出四元数非线性变换张量核范数（QNTTNN），通过实数嵌入解决四元数域张量补全问题，在彩色视频修复任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性变换张量核范数方法仅限于实值张量，无法处理彩色图像/视频中保持通道间依赖关系的四元数值数据，且四元数乘法的非交换性和奇异值分解的复杂性使其扩展困难。

Method: 通过实数嵌入将四元数映射到实域，定义可计算的四元数核范数；基于QNTTNN构建四元数张量补全模型；设计近端交替最小化算法并给出严格收敛性证明。

Result: 在基准彩色视频修复数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法在性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: QNTTNN有效突破了非线性变换TNN在四元数域的应用限制，为彩色多模态数据恢复提供了更强大的低秩建模能力。

Abstract: Tensor completion has emerged as a powerful framework for recovering missing data in multidimensional signals by exploiting low-rank tensor structures. Among existing approaches, linear transform-based tensor nuclear norm (TNN) methods have achieved considerable success by enforcing low-rankness on transformed frontal slices. However, the low-rank structure revealed by linear transforms remains inherently limited. To better capture intrinsic correlations, nonlinear transform-based TNN (NTTNN) models have been proposed, significantly enhancing low-rank representation through composite transforms. Despite their effectiveness, existing NTTNN methods are restricted to real-valued tensors and fail to model quaternion-valued data, which are essential for preserving inter-channel dependencies in color images and videos. Extending nonlinear TNN models to the quaternion domain is challenging due to the non-commutativity of quaternion multiplication and the complexity of quaternion singular value decomposition. To address the limitations encountered in prior works, we propose a quaternion nonlinear transform-induced tensor nuclear norm (QNTTNN) via a real embedding of quaternions, enabling tractable nuclear norm definitions and efficient optimization. Building upon QNTTNN, we formulate a quaternion tensor completion model and develop a proximal alternating minimization algorithm with rigorous convergence guarantees. Extensive experiments on benchmark color video inpainting datasets validate the superior performance of the proposed method over existing approaches.

</details>


### [188] [On the redundancy of transitivity constraints in the clique partitioning problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01481)
*Noriyoshi Sukegawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在团划分问题的0-1整数线性规划中识别了一类冗余传递性约束，这些约束虽定义多面体面但可被移除而不改变最优解集，从而得到更小且对相关聚类场景更有效的公式，计算实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过识别并移除团划分问题0-1整数线性规划中的冗余传递性约束，减小问题规模，提高求解效率，特别是针对相关聚类（边权重为{-1,1}）的实例。

Method: 分析团划分问题中传递性约束的冗余性，识别出一类虽定义多面体面但对最优解集无影响的约束，并将其从公式中移除，得到精简的整数线性规划公式。

Result: 精简后的公式在相关聚类实例上显著优于现有公式，计算实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 可以安全移除一类冗余传递性约束，从而得到更紧凑的公式，尤其适用于边权重为{-1,1}的相关聚类问题。

Abstract: In this study, we identify a class of redundant transitivity constraints in a 0-1 integer linear programming formulation of the clique partitioning problem. The transitivity constraints in this class can be removed from the formulation without changing the optimal solution set, although each transitivity constraint defines a facet of the associated polytope. This leads to a smaller formulation that is particularly effective for instances arising from correlation clustering, where edge weights are drawn from $\{-1,1\}$. Our computational experiments show that the resulting formulation outperforms existing formulations on such instances.

</details>


### [189] [On the convex hull of the graph of a simple monomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01493)
*Jon Lee,Daphne Skipper,Emily Speakman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了非负盒子域上简单单项式图的凸包，给出了线性不等式描述和体积公式。该域中至多一个变量的下界为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受先前在空间分支定界法中处理单项式的数学分析工作的启发，旨在为这一特定结构提供更精确的数学刻画。

Method: 通过数学推导，给出了凸包的线性不等式描述和体积计算公式。

Result: (i) 得到了凸包的一组线性不等式描述；(ii) 推导出了凸包的体积公式。

Conclusion: 为空间分支定界法中单项式的处理提供了更坚实的数学基础，有助于改进相关算法的理论分析。

Abstract: Motivated by previous efforts toward mathematically analyzing the treatment of monomials in spatial branch-and-bound, we study the convex hull of the graph of a simple monomial on a nonnegative box domain in arbitrary dimension, where at most one of the variable lower bounds is positive. We give: (i) a description via linear inequalities, and (ii) a formula for the volume.

</details>


### [190] [A Measure-Theoretic Formulation of Behavioral Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01558)
*Victor M. Preciado*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将行为系统理论从轨迹提升到轨迹上的概率测度，构建了凸且弱闭的行为-测度集，使得非线性和随机系统的优化变为线性规划。在此框架下建立了最优控制的线性规划对偶（等价于Bellman递归）和基于持久激励的数据驱动基本引理（测度层次），经典确定性结果作为Dirac测度的特例嵌入其中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Willems行为系统理论中，动力学系统被定义为所有与运动定律相容的轨迹集合。但对于非线性或随机系统，轨迹集通常非凸，阻碍了直接在行为上的优化。本文旨在通过将行为提升到轨迹概率测度空间来解决这一非凸性问题，使得即使是复杂动力学系统也能获得凸优化结构。

Method: 将有限时域动力学系统表示为所有支撑在其可容许轨迹上的Borel概率测度集合（行为-测度集），该集合在非线性或随机动力学下仍为凸且弱闭。其极点恰好是单个可容许轨迹上的Dirac测度，从而经典确定性理论作为该测度值对象的极值骨架嵌入。基于此建立：(1) 最优控制转化为占据测度上的线性规划，其对偶为Bellman动态规划递归；(2) 对可控线性时不变系统，证明测度层次的基本引理：有限时域行为上的任意概率测度可通过数据Hankel矩阵分解，将轨迹分布优化等价转化为系数空间分布优化。

Result: 建立了行为-测度集的凸性和弱闭性（定理核心）；最优控制线性规划与Bellman递归的强对偶性（在紧致性和连续性条件下）；测度层次的数据驱动基本引理——无需模型知识，仅需一条信息性轨迹即可实现精确的数据驱动重构，经典基本引理作为Dirac测度的特例。

Conclusion: 该测度层次框架为非线性与随机系统的数据驱动控制和优化提供了统一的凸分析基础，将确定性行为理论作为特例嵌入，并自然延伸至基于条件核一致性的随机扩展。

Abstract: In Willems' behavioral systems theory, a dynamical system is identified with the set of all trajectories compatible with its laws of motion. For nonlinear or stochastic systems, however, the admissible trajectory set is generally nonconvex, obstructing direct optimization over the behavior. In this paper, we lift the behavioral viewpoint from trajectories to probability measures on trajectories by representing a finite-horizon dynamical system with the set of all Borel probability measures supported on its admissible trajectories. This behavioral-measure set is convex and weakly closed even for nonlinear or stochastic dynamics, because convex combinations of trajectory distributions remain dynamically admissible even when convex combinations of trajectories do not. The extreme points are precisely the Dirac masses on individual admissible trajectories, so the classical deterministic theory is embedded as the extremal skeleton of the richer measure-valued object. On this foundation we establish two core deterministic results and outline a stochastic extension based on conditional kernel consistency. First, optimal control for a prescribed initial distribution becomes a linear program over occupation measures whose dual is exactly Bellman's dynamic-programming recursion, with strong duality under compactness and continuity. Second, for controllable linear time-invariant systems under persistency of excitation, we prove a measure-level Fundamental Lemma: every probability measure on the finite-horizon behavior factors through the data Hankel matrix, reducing any optimization over trajectory distributions to an equivalent optimization over coefficient-space distributions. This is an exact data-driven reformulation requiring no model knowledge beyond a single informative trajectory; the classical Fundamental Lemma is recovered as the special case of Dirac measures.

</details>


### [191] [Bilevel learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01621)
*Riccardo Grazzi,Massimiliano Pontil,Saverio Salzo,Alain Zemkoho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了双层学习（bilevel learning）领域的最新算法进展，指出基于隐函数定理的梯度方法虽然实现了大规模问题的求解，但存在下层问题解唯一性和约束处理方面的局限性。文章通过连接经典双层优化文献，探讨了克服这些限制的可扩展算法方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前双层学习算法基于隐函数微分框架，但该框架依赖下层问题解唯一性等限制性假设，且当下层包含约束时梯度计算复杂，导致现有算法仅适用于较窄的问题类别。本文旨在弥合双层学习与经典双层优化之间的鸿沟，推动开发能求解更一般大规模双层规划的扩展方法。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性的方法论文，其"方法"在于：(1) 系统梳理基于隐函数定理的双层学习算法核心机制及其可扩展性来源；(2) 分析这些算法在更一般设置（如下层问题非唯一解、含约束）下的局限性；(3) 建立与经典双层优化文献（如最优性条件、KKT条件、值函数方法等）的联系；(4) 讨论可能克服当前限制的算法技术。

Result: 本文识别出当前双层学习算法的关键局限性：下层问题解唯一性假设、约束处理复杂度高等。通过连接经典双层优化理论，指出可利用KKT条件重构、值函数方法、罚函数法等经典技术来扩展可解问题类别，为开发更通用的可扩展双层优化算法提供了理论方向和文献基础。

Conclusion: 本文强调了将现代双层学习的可扩展算法技术与经典双层优化的丰富理论工具相结合的重要性，认为这是突破当前限制、发展能处理更一般大规模双层规划（如下层非唯一解、带约束）的可扩展方法的关键路径。

Abstract: Bilevel learning refers to machine learning problems that can be formulated as bilevel optimization models, where decisions are organized in a hierarchical structure. This paradigm has recently gained considerable attention in machine learning, as gradient-based algorithms built on the implicit function reformulation have enabled the computation of large-scale problems involving possibly millions of variables. Despite these advances, the implicit function framework relies on restrictive assumptions, notably the requirement that the lower-level problem admit a unique optimal solution for each upper-level decision. Moreover, the computation of the derivative of the lower-level optimal solution function becomes significantly more involved when the lower-level problem includes constraints. As a result, many existing bilevel learning algorithms are effective only for relatively narrow classes of problems. This paper reviews the main algorithmic ideas underlying recent progress in bilevel learning, highlighting both the key mechanisms responsible for their scalability and the limitations that arise in more general settings. We then draw connections with the broader bilevel optimization literature and discuss algorithmic techniques that may help overcome these limitations. Our aim is to bridge the gap between bilevel learning and classical bilevel optimization, thereby supporting the development of scalable methods capable of solving more general large-scale bilevel programs.

</details>


### [192] [Optimal transport between laws of random probability measures and the strict Monge problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01816)
*Alessandro Pinzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机概率测度之间的最优传输问题，提出了基于随机耦合的新型OT框架，刻画了最优随机耦合的Kantorovich势表征，并在严格Monge问题下给出了与Brenier定理类似的唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输理论处理的是概率测度之间的传输，但许多实际问题（如贝叶斯推断、粒子滤波、随机过程）中需要处理的是"概率测度上的概率测度"（即随机概率测度）。本文旨在建立这种高阶概率测度之间的最优传输理论。

Method: (1) 以基础成本函数为起点，定义概率测度间的OT成本，进而构建概率测度上的概率测度之间的OT成本；(2) 引入"随机耦合"（随机测度的联合分布）替代普通耦合，保留更多信息；(3) 用Kantorovich势刻画最优随机耦合；(4) 引入严格Monge问题，其约束比传统Monge问题更苛刻；(5) 在严格凸Banach空间中距离的p次幂（p>1）作为成本时，给出最优随机耦合唯一性条件及其由严格Monge问题解诱导的条件。

Result: (1) 给出了最优随机耦合的Kantorovich势表征；(2) 证明了在适当条件下，严格Monge问题与传统随机耦合OT问题价值相等（类似Pratelli的结果）；(3) 在严格凸Banach空间中，当成本为距离的p次幂（p>1）时，证明了最优随机耦合的唯一性及其由严格Monge问题解诱导的性质（类似Brenier定理）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了随机概率测度间的OT理论框架，其中随机耦合比普通耦合更具信息量；通过Kantorovich势刻画了最优性，并在严格Monge问题下推广了经典Brenier定理到这一高阶设定，为后续在贝叶斯推断、随机分析等领域的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider an optimal transport problem between laws of random probability measures: given a base cost function, we build the associated OT cost between probability measures that in turn we use to define the OT cost between probability measures over probability measures. This setting admits a finer reformulation in terms of laws of random couplings, which retain more information than ordinary couplings. One of the main contributions of the paper is the characterization of the optimal ones in terms of Kantorovich potentials.
  Similarly, we also introduce the strict Monge problem, whose admissible competitors are more restrictive than in the usual Monge formulation. In this setting, we will give sufficient conditions under which the value of this problem is the same as the one considered above, in the spirit of the result by A. Pratelli. Then, for $p>1$, when the underlying cost is the distance to the power $p$ in a strictly convex Banach space, we will give sufficient conditions under which the optimal random coupling is unique and induced by a solution of the strict Monge problem, resembling the Brenier theorem.

</details>


### [193] [Fast Newton methods for linear-quadratic dynamic games with application to autonomous vehicle platooning and intersection crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01898)
*Reza Rahimi Baghbadorani,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶车队编队、交叉口通行等协同驾驶场景中的约束线性二次动态博弈问题，将无限时域纳什均衡重新表述为具有特殊结构的滚动时域仿射变分不等式，并设计了具有局部二次收敛速度的牛顿型算法。该方法收敛极快，适用于安全关键交通场景的实时和嵌入式滚动时域控制。车队和交叉口仿真表明，该方法相比一阶和算子分裂方法具有显著性能优势和应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车队编队和交叉口通行等协同驾驶场景中存在约束线性二次动态博弈，传统一阶和算子分裂方法收敛速度慢，难以满足安全关键交通场景对实时性和嵌入式控制的需求。

Method: 将无限时域纳什均衡重新表述为滚动时域仿射变分不等式，并利用其特殊结构设计具有局部二次收敛速度的牛顿型算法。

Result: 所提牛顿型算法收敛极快，在车队编队和交叉口通行仿真中相比一阶和算子分裂方法表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于安全关键交通场景的实时和嵌入式滚动时域控制，具有很高的应用潜力。

Abstract: We consider constrained linear-quadratic dynamic games arising in autonomous vehicle platooning, intersection crossing and other cooperative driving scenarios. Infinite-horizon Nash equilibria are reformulated as receding-horizon affine variational inequalities with special structure. Exploiting this formulation, we design Newton-type algorithms with local quadratic convergence. The resulting methods achieve extremely fast convergence, making them well suited for real-time and embedded receding-horizon control in safety-critical traffic applications. Simulations of platooning and intersection crossing demonstrate substantial performance gains over first-order and operator-splitting approaches, hence high application potential.

</details>


### [194] [The Control Plant as A Communication Channel: Implicit Communication for Decentralized LQG Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01903)
*Gongpu Chen,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对领导者与跟随者之间的非对称信息（目标状态仅领导者知晓且无显式通信信道）的线性二次型高斯控制问题，提出了一种集成通信与控制（ICoCo）框架。该框架将被控对象本身作为通信信道：领导者通过附加通信项将目标状态编码到控制输入中，跟随者从状态轨迹中解码。基于联合信源信道编码的隐式协调方案被证明可使跟随者的估计误差单调递减至零，最终实现系统镇定。通信功率设计被建模为最优控制问题，在完全驱动情况下导出必要条件，在欠驱动情况下进行数值求解。数值结果表明，所提方案接近显式通信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式控制系统中，当领导者与跟随者之间存在信息不对称（目标状态仅领导者知晓）且不存在显式通信信道时，如何实现有效协调是一个关键挑战。现有方法通常依赖独立通信信道，但这一假设在资源受限或不可靠环境中可能不成立。本文旨在探索利用被控对象本身作为通信介质的可能性，从而实现控制与通信的一体化设计。

Method: 提出集成通信与控制（ICoCo）框架，采用联合信源信道编码思想设计隐式协调方案。领导者通过控制输入中添加通信项对目标状态进行编码，跟随者从状态轨迹中解码出目标信息。将通信功率设计建模为最优控制问题：完全驱动情况下利用最优控制理论推导必要条件，欠驱动情况下采用数值方法求解。

Result: 理论证明跟随者的估计误差单调递减至零，系统最终可被镇定至目标状态。数值结果表明，所提方案的控制成本接近具有显式通信信道（即信息完全共享）的下界。

Conclusion: 利用被控对象本身作为通信信道是解决非对称信息下分布式控制问题的有效途径，能够在无显式通信信道条件下实现接近完美通信的性能，为通信受限环境下的协同控制提供了一种新的设计范式。

Abstract: We study a decentralized linear quadratic Gaussian control problem, in which a leader and a follower must steer a linear system to a target state. The target state is known only to the leader, and no explicit communication channel exists between the agents. To address the challenge posed by this asymmetric information structure, we propose an integrated communication and control (ICoCo) framework in which the control plant itself serves as a communication channel: the leader encodes the target state into its control input through an additive communication term, and the follower decodes it from the resulting state trajectory. We design an implicit coordination scheme based on joint source-channel coding ideas, and prove that the follower's estimation error decreases monotonically to zero, enabling the two agents to coordinate increasingly well and ultimately steer the system to the target state. We then formulate the design of the communication power as an optimal control problem to minimize the overall control cost. In the fully actuated leader case, we derive necessary optimality conditions and in the under-actuated case, we solve the problem numerically. Numerical results show that the proposed scheme effectively coordinates the two agents and achieves a control cost close to that of the explicit-communication lower bound.

</details>


### [195] [A Parameter-Free First-Order Algorithm for Non-Convex Optimization with $\tilde{\mkern1mu O}(ε^{-5/3})$ Global Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02127)
*Sichao Xiong,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出PF-AGD，首个无参数、确定性的加速一阶方法，在光滑非凸函数上达到O(ε^{-5/3}log(1/ε))的预言复杂度，这是当前最优界。该方法通过自适应回溯方案和基于梯度的重启机制估计局部曲率，无需先验知识，理论上匹配最优速率，实验上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速一阶方法虽能达到最优复杂度，但需要预先知道光滑性常数，这在实践中难以获取。作者希望设计一种无需超参数调优、能自动适应局部曲率的确定性加速方法，同时保持理论最优复杂度。

Method: 1) 自适应回溯(backtracking)方案：动态估计局部光滑性常数；2) 基于梯度的重启机制：当梯度信号指示曲率变化时重置动量；3) 结合这两种机制形成无参数加速梯度下降框架。

Result: 1) 理论：达到O(ε^{-5/3}log(1/ε))的预言复杂度，匹配已知最优界；2) 实验： outperforms AGD-Until-Guilty的实用变体及其他无参数变体，可作为非线性共轭梯度法的可行替代方案。

Conclusion: PF-AGD是首个无需超参数即可达到非凸光滑优化最优理论速率的一阶方法，兼具理论保证与实践有效性。

Abstract: We introduce PF-AGD, the first parameter-free, deterministic, accelerated first-order method to achieve $O(ε^{-5/3}\log(1/ε))$ oracle complexity bound when minimizing sufficiently smooth, non-convex functions; this is the best-known bound for first-order methods on smooth non-convex objectives. Unlike existing methods possessing this rate that require a priori knowledge of smoothness constants, we use an adaptive backtracking scheme and a gradient-based restart mechanism to estimate local curvature. This yields a practical algorithm that matches best-known theoretical rates. Empirically, PF-AGD outperforms the practical variant of AGD-Until-Guilty (Carmon et al., 2017), as well as other parameter-free variants, and is a viable alternative to nonlinear conjugate gradient methods.

</details>


### [196] [A Theory of Composition and Duality of Extremal Optimal Fixed-Point Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02231)
*TaeHo Yoon,Benjamin Grimmer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非扩张不动点问题中精确极小极大最优算法背后的丰富组合结构，通过下三角矩阵H和弧图（arc diagram）统一了所有极值最优方法，并基于此设计了具有准任意时间保证（quasi-anytime guarantees）的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非扩张不动点问题的最优算法在理论上已有较多研究，但不同最优算法之间的内在联系和组合结构尚未被系统揭示。作者希望从组合视角统一现有极值最优方法，并建立可系统设计与分析新算法的框架。

Method: 将固定步长算法表示为下三角矩阵H，证明最优(N-1)步算法集合恰好有(N-1)!个顶点（极值算法），每个顶点自然对应一个弧图（arc diagram），该图编码了算法的收敛性证明。引入H-对偶操作（反斜线转置），分析其保持顶点算法最优性的条件，并刻画对偶算法的收敛性证明。

Result: 建立了极值最优算法与弧图之间的一一对应关系；明确了H-对偶操作保持最优性的条件；基于该组合框架，构造了具有准任意时间保证的新最优算法，且算法对违反非扩张性的不动点算子具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 组合视角为理解和设计非扩张不动点问题的最优算法提供了统一且实用的工具，所提出的弧图方法可系统地进行算法的组合、分解与性质分析，并能够设计出适应实际需求（如任意时间停止和鲁棒性）的新算法。

Abstract: In this work, we reveal a rich combinatorial structure underlying exact minimax optimal algorithms for classical nonexpansive fixed-point problems. This viewpoint unifies all extremal optimal methods and provides a systematic and practical framework for designing new algorithms via diagrams. Specifically, we study fixed-step algorithms represented by a lower triangular matrix H, and show that the set of optimal (N-1)-step algorithms has exactly (N-1)! vertices (extremal algorithms), each of which naturally corresponds to an arc diagram, a graph that encodes its convergence proof. Using these arc diagrams, we can compose, decompose, and analyze the properties of distinct optimal vertex algorithms. Furthermore, we determine when the H-dual operation, given by taking the anti-diagonal transpose of H, preserves the optimality of a vertex algorithm, and in such cases we characterize the convergence proof of the dual algorithm. Based on this machinery, we develop new optimal algorithms with quasi-anytime guarantees; that is, they admit an increasing integer sequence such that the corresponding iterates have the optimal residual guarantees, and are additionally robust to fixed-point operators that violate nonexpansiveness.

</details>


### [197] [A computational comparison of handling distance constraints in MINLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02305)
*Christopher Hojny,Leo Liberti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对含有最小距离约束（minDCs）的几何优化问题，提出了新的变量边界收紧算法，并利用Givens旋转引入词典序约束来处理旋转对称性，通过计算实验评估了各方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小距离约束（minDCs）出现在许多几何优化问题中，但由于其反凸性，给混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）带来了重大挑战。此外，这类问题通常具有显著的对称性，进一步增加了求解难度。

Method: 1) 开发了针对含minDCs的通用MINLP的变量边界收紧新算法；2) 提出通过Givens旋转诱导的词典序约束分离来处理旋转对称性的实用方法。

Result: 通过计算研究检验了各方法的性能，确定了每种方法表现优越的具体场景。

Conclusion: 提出的边界收紧算法和对称性处理方法能有效应对含最小距离约束的MINLP问题，且不同方法在不同场景下各有优势。

Abstract: Minimum distance constraints (minDCs) appear in many geometric optimization problems. They pose major challenges for mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) due to their reverse-convexity. We develop new algorithms for tightening variable bounds in general MINLPs with minDCs. Because many such problems exhibit substantial symmetry, we further introduce a practical approach for handling rotation symmetries via separation of lexicographic constraints induced by Givens rotations. In a computational study, we examine the performance of the various methods and determine the scenarios in which each approach demonstrates superiority.

</details>


### [198] [Improved semidefinite programming bounds for the maximum $k$-colorable subgraph problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02456)
*Mathijs Barkel,Renata Sotirov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对最大k-可着色子图(MkCS)问题，提出了基于半定规划(SDP)松弛的优化框架。通过分析SDP松弛的图参数性质，证明了MkCS问题在k-完美图上可多项式时间求解。引入两类有效不等式加强松弛（分别推广了布尔二次多面体和稳定集问题的秩不等式），并设计了基于交替方向乘子法(ADMM)的割平面算法高效求解。计算实验表明所得上界优于现有文献最佳上界。此外，首次将ADMM应用于精确二元半定规划(BSDP)公式以生成高质量可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MkCS问题是图论与组合优化中的经典NP难问题，现有上界方法精度有限。作者希望通过SDP松弛及其加强来获得更紧的上界，并探索多项式可解的特殊图类，同时开发高效算法以处理大规模实例。

Method: (1) 建立MkCS问题的SDP松弛并将其上界视为图参数，分析其性质（如单调性、子图可加性等）；(2) 证明该图参数在k-完美图上等于整数最优值，从而导出多项式可解性；(3) 引入两类有效不等式：第一类在k=1时退化为布尔二次多面体不等式，第二类推广了稳定集问题的秩不等式；(4) 设计基于ADMM的割平面算法求解加强后的SDP松弛；(5) 提出整数ADMM变体，直接求解MkCS问题的精确BSDP公式以获得可行解。

Result: 理论结果表明MkCS问题在k-完美图上多项式时间可解；数值实验验证了加强SDP松弛产生的上界全面优于现有文献中的最佳上界；整数ADMM变体成功计算出高质量可行解，且是ADMM在BSDP问题上的首次应用。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和算法两个层面推进了MkCS问题的求解：理论上揭示了其与k-完美图及SDP图参数的内在联系，实践上提供了业界领先的上下界计算框架。ADMM在BSDP中的成功应用也为相关二元组合优化问题开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We study the maximum $k$-colorable subgraph (M$k$CS) problem, which consists in finding a largest $k$-colorable induced subgraph in a given graph. We consider a Semidefinite Programming (SDP) relaxation for the M$k$CS problem and regard its resulting upper bound as a graph parameter. We present several properties of this graph parameter, from which we obtain that the M$k$CS problem is solvable in polynomial time for $k$-perfect graphs. We further derive two novel families of valid inequalities to strengthen the SDP relaxation. The first family reduces to a family of inequalities for the Boolean quadric polytope when $k = 1$, and the second family generalizes the family of rank inequalities for binary linear programming formulations of the stable set problem. We efficiently solve the strengthened SDP relaxation using a cutting-plane algorithm that is based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM). Extensive computational experiments show that the obtained upper bounds outperform the best upper bounds from the literature. To complement our SDP-based upper bounds, we propose an integer ADMM variant that uses an exact Binary Semidefinite Programming (BSDP) formulation of the M$k$CS problem to produce high-quality feasible solutions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of the ADMM to compute integer solutions to a BSDP problem.

</details>


### [199] [Closed Forms for Gaussian Kullback--Leibler Unbalanced Optimal Transport without Coupling Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02497)
*Jiaping Yang,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了有限非退化高斯测度之间静态KL非平衡最优传输问题的显式解，其中使用二次成本、两个独立的正边际松弛参数，且耦合上无熵惩罚。最小化器是两个调整后的高斯边际之间的缩放Wasserstein耦合，支撑在仿射图上；与熵高斯非平衡传输不同，最优计划在乘积空间上是非退化的。协方差映射是Riccati方程的唯一正定解，并具有主平方根表示。与已知的等惩罚高斯Hellinger-Kantorovich端点相比，该结果处理了非对称双边KL松弛，给出了修正的边际、联合最小化器、值以及直接的二次KL对偶证书。大松弛极限在等质量情况下恢复了高斯Wasserstein成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高斯非平衡最优传输研究主要集中在对称的等惩罚Hellinger-Kantorovich设置上，缺乏对非对称双边KL松弛的显式解。本文旨在填补这一空白，为有限非退化高斯测度之间带有两个独立边际松弛参数的静态KL非平衡最优传输问题提供完整显式解。

Method: 通过求解Riccati方程获得协方差映射的唯一正定解，利用主平方根表示构造最小化器。该最小化器是缩放Wasserstein耦合，支撑在仿射图上，同时推导了二次KL对偶证书和修正边际的显式表达式。

Result: 得到了显式的最小化器（缩放Wasserstein耦合，支撑在仿射图上）、修正的高斯边际、最优值以及直接的二次KL对偶证书。协方差映射是Riccati方程的唯一正定解，具有主平方根表示。大松弛极限下恢复等质量的高斯Wasserstein成本。

Conclusion: 本文成功将非对称双边KL松弛引入高斯非平衡最优传输，提供了完整的显式解，推广了等惩罚高斯Hellinger-Kantorovich结果，并揭示了与熵正则化高斯非平衡传输的本质区别。

Abstract: We obtain an explicit solution for the static Kullback--Leibler (KL) unbalanced optimal transport problem between finite non-degenerate Gaussian measures with quadratic cost, two independent positive marginal relaxation parameters, and no entropy penalty on the coupling. The minimizer is a scaled Wasserstein coupling between two adjusted Gaussian marginals and is supported on an affine graph; in entropic Gaussian unbalanced transport, by contrast, the optimal plan is non-degenerate on the product space. The covariance map is the unique positive definite solution of a Riccati equation and admits a principal-square-root representation. Compared with the known equal-penalty Gaussian Hellinger--Kantorovich endpoint, the result treats the asymmetric two-sided Kullback--Leibler relaxation and gives the modified marginals, joint minimizer, value, and a direct quadratic KL-dual certificate. The large-relaxation limit recovers the Gaussian Wasserstein cost for equal masses.

</details>


### [200] [Linear Decision Tree Policies for Integer Linear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02582)
*Théo Guyard,Cleber Oliveira,Maximilian Schiffer,Eduardo Uchoa,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究整数线性规划的最优决策策略，通过线性决策树为固定可行集预计算策略，使得对于任意变化的成本向量，只需多项式次算术运算即可得到最优解。实验表明，尽管策略合成耗时，但在重复查询场景下求解速度比传统方法快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统整数线性规划方法在每次成本向量变化时都需要从头求解，效率低下。作者希望设计一种离线-在线框架，通过预先为固定可行集合成决策策略，使得在线查询时能极快速地获得最优解。

Method: 将整数线性规划的最优决策问题建模为线性决策树，为固定可行集合成一个策略，该策略通过一系列线性测试对任意查询的成本向量导向最优解。作者理论证明了最坏情况下存在多项式时间策略，并开发了实际合成框架用于特定子类线性决策树的构建。

Result: 理论上证明了存在最坏情况下多项式次算术运算即可求解的策略。实验表明，虽然策略合成阶段耗时较长，但在重复查询时，检索最优解的速度比经典方法和专用求解方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该研究为整数线性规划的复杂性问题提供了新视角，并提出了一个有效的离线-在线求解范式，兼顾了理论保证与实际计算效率。

Abstract: We study optimal decision policies for integer linear programs with a fixed feasible set and varying cost vectors, represented as linear decision trees. Once synthesized for a given feasible set, they return an optimal solution for any queried cost vector through a sequence of linear tests. We show that there exists a policy performing this operation in a polynomial number of arithmetic operations in the worst case. Along with this theoretical guarantee, we develop a practical construction framework to synthesize policies within a specific subclass of linear decision trees. Our computational experiments show that, although policy synthesis can be time-intensive, it allows retrieving optimal solutions orders of magnitude faster than classical and specialized solution methods on repeated queries. Overall, this paradigm provides a new perspective on the complexity of integer linear programs and offers an offline--online approach for solving them.

</details>


### [201] [Mirror Descent for Deterministic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02653)
*Ye Feng,Jianfeng Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究面向有限时域确定性最优控制问题的显式镜像下降方法，基于庞特里亚金最大值原理构建迭代框架，在欧几里得情形退化为投影梯度法。在全局光滑性和一致凸性假设下，证明了代价泛函的相对光滑性估计和能量耗散不等式；在未正则化Hamiltonian满足凹性且终端代价凸性的条件下，建立了正则化目标的相对凸性。由此导出未正则化凸情形下O(1/n)的收敛率，以及控制正则化参数为正时的几何收敛率。数值实验涵盖线性二次型、退化凸和非线性高维场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优控制问题的数值方法（如直接法/间接法）在大规模或非线性场景下存在计算瓶颈，需要一种兼具理论保证和计算效率的显式一阶方法。庞特里亚金最大值原理提供了梯度的解析结构，但直接应用梯度下降缺乏收敛性分析。

Method: 提出显式镜像下降方法：每次迭代求解状态方程和伴随方程，通过最大化利用Bregman散度惩罚的正则化Hamiltonian的一阶近似来更新控制量。欧几里得情形下等价于控制变量的投影梯度步。理论分析基于相对光滑性（相对Lipschitz梯度）和相对凸性框架。

Result: 证明了：1) 代价泛函满足相对光滑性估计，小步长下具有能量耗散不等式；2) 在Hamiltonian凹性和终端代价凸性下，正则化目标满足相对凸性；3) 未正则化凸情形收敛率O(1/n)；4) 正正则化参数时实现几何收敛率。

Conclusion: 显式镜像下降法为一类最优控制问题提供了可证明收敛的一阶优化框架，在线性二次型、退化凸及非线性高维场景中均有效，拓展了镜像下降在控制领域的理论边界。

Abstract: We study an explicit mirror-descent method for finite-horizon deterministic optimal control problems. The method is motivated by Pontryagin's maximum principle: at each iteration, one solves the state and adjoint equations and updates the control by maximizing a first-order approximation of the regularized Hamiltonian penalized by a Bregman divergence. In the Euclidean case, the update reduces to a projected gradient step in the control variable. Under global smoothness assumptions and uniform convexity of the mirror map, we prove a relative smoothness estimate for the cost functional and derive an energy dissipation inequality for sufficiently small step sizes. Under an additional concavity assumption on the unregularized Hamiltonian and convexity of the terminal cost, we establish relative convexity of the regularized objective. These estimates yield an $O(1/n)$ convergence rate in the unregularized convex case and a geometric rate when the control regularization parameter is positive. Numerical examples illustrate the behavior of the method in linear-quadratic, degenerate convex, and nonlinear high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [202] [Robust and Fast Training via Per-Sample Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02701)
*Davide Nobile,Philipp Grohs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于逐样本梯度裁剪的鲁棒梯度估计器（PS-Clip-SGD），理论证明其在重尾噪声非凸优化中达到期望最优收敛率，并建立高概率收敛保证。实验表明PS-Clip-SGD在CIFAR-100上训练AlexNet优于带动量的SGD和标准梯度裁剪，且发现小批量级别裁剪在梯度累积场景下可提升性能且几乎无额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度裁剪方法大多在批量层级进行裁剪，无法有效应对重尾梯度噪声，收敛理论分析不足。本文旨在设计一种基于逐样本梯度裁剪的鲁棒优化器，提供理论收敛保证并改善实际训练效果。

Method: 提出逐样本裁剪SGD（PS-Clip-SGD），对每个样本的梯度单独进行裁剪后再聚合；开展期望收敛率和高概率收敛边界的理论分析；在CIFAR-100上使用AlexNet进行实验验证，并探索梯度累积场景下不同裁剪策略的效果。

Result: PS-Clip-SGD在重尾噪声非凸优化下达到最优期望收敛率；高概率收敛保证与期望收敛率仅差多对数因子；在CIFAR-100/AlexNet任务上优于带动量SGD和标准梯度裁剪；梯度累积时小批量级裁剪优于累积后裁剪，且几乎无额外计算成本。

Conclusion: 逐样本梯度裁剪是应对重尾噪声的有效鲁棒优化策略，在理论和实践上均优于传统批量级裁剪；梯度累积场景下的裁剪时机选择值得重新审视。

Abstract: We propose a robust gradient estimator based on per-sample gradient clipping and analyze its properties both theoretically and empirically. We show that the resulting method, per-sample clipped SGD (PS-Clip-SGD), achieves optimal in-expectation convergence rates for non-convex optimization problems under heavy-tailed gradient noise. Moreover, we establish high-probability convergence guarantees that match the in-expectation rates up to polylogarithmic factors in the failure probability. We complement our theoretical results with multiple numerical experiments. In particular, we demonstrate that PS-Clip-SGD outperforms both vanilla SGD with momentum and standard gradient clipping when training AlexNet on the CIFAR-100 dataset, even after accounting for the additional computational time caused by per-sample clipping. We also empirically show that, in the presence of gradient accumulation, applying clipping at the mini-batch level can improve training performance while incurring virtually no additional computational cost. This finding is particularly interesting, as it contradicts the common practice of applying clipping only after all accumulation steps have been completed.

</details>


### [203] [Identifiability and Error Bound: Metric and Geometric Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02754)
*Hanju Wu,Yue Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在欧氏空间中，局部误差界（EB）性质在全局空间与可识别流形上是等价的，并从度量和几何两个角度给出了证明，为无约束优化中可识别性分析提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可识别性保证优化算法迭代最终被限制在某个可识别集合上，从而将非光滑函数的极小化问题简化为其在可识别流形上的光滑限制。受此简化启发，作者希望从流形视角重新研究误差界性质。

Method: 从两个互补视角建立等价性：(1) 基于斜率和远离流形方向的线性增长的度量分析；(2) 基于次微分、部分光滑性和 $\mathcal{VU}$-理论的几何分析。

Result: 在欧氏空间的温和假设下，证明了 $(\mathbb{R}^n,d)$ 上的局部EB等价于可识别流形 $(\mathcal{M},d)$ 上的局部EB，并作为应用恢复了 $\ell_1$ 正则化优化中已有的EB等价性结果。

Conclusion: 该等价性为利用可识别流形简化非光滑优化问题的误差界分析提供了理论保证，具有重要的算法分析价值。

Abstract: Identifiability means that iterates generated by optimization algorithms are eventually confined to an identifiable set. This property is computationally useful because minimizing a nonsmooth function near a critical point reduces to minimizing its smooth restriction on the corresponding identifiable manifold. Motivated by this reduction, we study the Error Bound (EB) property from both ambient and manifold viewpoints. Under mild assumptions in Euclidean space, we prove that local EB on $(\mathbb{R}^n,d)$ is equivalent to local EB on an identifiable manifold $(\mathcal{M},d)$. We establish this result from two complementary perspectives: a metric analysis based on slope and linear growth away from $\mathcal{M}$, and a geometric analysis based on subdifferentials, partial smoothness, and $\mathcal{VU}$-theory. As an application, we recover the EB equivalence for $\ell_1$-regularized optimization in the literature.

</details>


### [204] [Risk-Averse Ensemble Control for Control-Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02791)
*Alessandro Scagliotti,Thomas M. Surowiec*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机动力系统的风险厌恶型集值控制问题，建立了控制-状态映射的弱-强连续性、Frechet可微性及导数算子连续性等理论框架，并推导了基于有界变差伴随状态的一阶最优性条件，通过量子控制数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准集值控制方法仅通过期望处理目标函数中的不确定性，得到的最优控制仅在平均意义上有效，而忽略了关键的异常现象（outlier phenomena），因此需要发展风险厌恶型集值控制的理论框架。

Method: 采用控制仿射结构确保下半连续性以证明最优解存在性；严格刻画控制-状态映射，建立弱-强连续性、连续Frechet可微性以及导数算子的弱-强连续性；基于上述正则性推导由有界变差伴随状态表征的一阶最优性条件。

Result: 建立了控制-状态映射的弱-强连续性、连续Frechet可微性及导数算子弱-强连续性；推导了基于有界变差伴随状态的一阶最优性条件；满足无限维优化算法收敛所需的功能性前提；通过量子控制数值实验验证了理论成果。

Conclusion: 本文为风险厌恶型集值控制提供了完整的数学处理，所建立的正则性理论为一阶最优性条件和优化算法收敛性奠定了基础，数值实验验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: A number of important modern applications in optimal control can be formulated as open loop control problems in which the underlying dynamical systems are subject to random inputs. These so-called ensemble control problems require the corresponding optimal control to be deterministic, as it must be computed before the realization of uncertainty and the passage of time. Practical applications of ensemble control include quantum control and the training of Neural ODEs. However, the standard approach to ensemble control treats the uncertainty in the objective function via the expectation, which provides optimal controls that only work well on average while ignoring critical outlier phenomena. This study provides a comprehensive mathematical treatment of risk-averse ensemble control. Within this setting, we adopt a control-affine structure that ensures the lower semi-continuity needed for proving the existence of optimal solutions. The central analytical contribution of this paper is a rigorous characterization of the control-to-state mapping in which we establish weak-to-strong continuity, continuous Fréchet differentiability, and weak-to-strong continuity of the derivative operator. Furthermore, this regularity yields primal and dual first-order optimality conditions characterized by an adjoint state of bounded variation, and it fulfills the functional prerequisites required for the convergence of infinite dimensional optimization algorithms. We conclude by validating these theoretical developments through a numerical experiment in quantum control.

</details>


### [205] [Statistical Inference of Day-to-Day Traffic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02806)
*Minghui Wu,Yafeng Yin,Jerome P. Lynch,Zhichen Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机个体层面调整模型的日间路径选择动力学统计推断框架，支持从轨迹数据中进行行为参数的不确定性量化和形式推断，并通过仿真和实证验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的日间交通动力学模型多依赖描述性标定，缺乏统计推断能力，无法对行为参数进行不确定性量化和形式化推断。

Method: 构建了随机个体层面的日间路径选择调整模型，建立了统计推断框架；在温和条件下证明了可识别性与一致性；扩展了框架以容纳需求变化、用户异质性（层次结构）以及因隐私约束导致的匿名可观测性。

Result: 仿真研究表明框架具有良好有限样本性能、校准后的不确定性以及对模型误设的鲁棒性。实证分析（受控实验室实验和密歇根州安阿伯市真实轨迹数据）揭示了：（1）引入途中信息后纯日间学习模型不足以描述行为；（2）不同参与者类型之间存在系统性行为差异；（3）真实通勤行为中存在显著的日间学习效应及需求变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为日间路径选择动态提供了可靠的统计推断工具，能够生成跨场景的新颖行为洞察，有望推动交通行为建模从描述性分析向推断性分析转型。

Abstract: Day-to-day traffic dynamics are widely used to model flow evolution due to travelers' learning and adjustment behavior, yet empirical analysis of these models often relies on descriptive calibration with limited inferential content. This paper develops a statistical inference framework for day-to-day route choice dynamics based on a stochastic individual-level adjustment model. The framework enables uncertainty quantification and formal inference for behavioral parameters from trajectory data. We establish identifiability and consistency under mild conditions, and extend the framework to accommodate demand variation, user heterogeneity through a hierarchical structure, and anonymized observability caused by privacy constraints on trajectory data. Simulation studies demonstrate good finite-sample performance, calibrated uncertainty, and robustness to model misspecification. Empirical analyses of controlled laboratory experiments and real-world trajectory data from Ann Arbor, Michigan, show that the framework can generate novel behavioral insights across settings: it reveals the inadequacy of a purely inter-day learning model once en-route information is introduced, recovers systematic behavioral differences across participant types, and uncovers meaningful day-to-day learning together with substantial demand variation in real-world commuting behavior.

</details>


### [206] [Null Controllability for a Multi-Dimensional Degenerate Parabolic Equation with Degenerated Interior Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02830)
*Dong-Hui Yang,Bao-Zhu Guo,Jie Zhong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有内部退化点的多维退化抛物方程在控制域不包含退化点时的零能控性，通过用一致椭圆PDE逼近退化PDE并推导Carleman估计和可观测性不等式来证明结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决控制域不包含退化点时，多维退化抛物方程的零能控性问题，填补现有研究空白。

Method: 采用新的逼近方法，用一系列一致椭圆PDE逼近退化PDE，进而推导一致抛物方程的Carleman估计并建立可观测性不等式。

Result: 成功建立了可观测性不等式，并证明了系统的零能控性。

Conclusion: 该逼近方法有效解决了控制域不包含退化点情况下的零能控性问题，为退化PDE控制理论提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this study, we study the null controllability of a multi-dimensional degenerate parabolic equation characterized by a degenerate interior point. The control domain, which is an arbitrary inner region, does not encompass the degenerate point. To tackle this problem, we adopt a new approximation methodology. Specifically, we approximate the degenerate partial differential equations (PDEs) with a series of uniformly elliptic PDEs, notwithstanding their limited regularity. We then derive the Carleman estimate for these approximate uniformly parabolic equations and establish the observability inequality, which ultimately paves the way for demonstrating the null controllability of the system.

</details>


### [207] [A second-order method on the Stiefel manifold via Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02838)
*Xinhui Xiong,Bin Gao,P. -A. Absil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需回缩（retraction-free）的Stiefel流形二阶优化方法，其更新由切向（降低目标函数）和法向（减少不可行性）两部分组成，其中法向分量通过Newton–Schulz迭代实现正交化，并建立了Newton–Schulz与Stiefel流形法空间的几何联系。实验表明该方法在正交Procrustes问题、PCA和ICA上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Stiefel流形无回缩方法通常仅为一阶方法，在高精度需求下效率受限；而带回缩的黎曼方法计算成本较高。因此，作者希望设计一种无需回缩的二阶方法，兼顾低计算成本与高精度收敛速度。

Method: 将更新分解为切向分量（通过修改的牛顿方程求解，用于降低目标函数）和法向分量（通过Newton–Schulz定点迭代实现正交化，用于减少约束违例），并证明了Newton–Schulz迭代实质上是在Stiefel流形的法空间中移动。

Result: 理论分析证明该方法的精确变体具有局部二次收敛，非精确变体具有局部超线性收敛。在正交Procrustes问题、主成分分析和真实数据独立成分分析上的数值实验表明，所提方法优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 成功设计并验证了一种无需回缩的Stiefel流形二阶优化方法，在保持低计算成本的同时实现了快速收敛，为流形优化提供了新的高效路径。

Abstract: Retraction-free approaches offer attractive low-cost alternatives to Riemannian methods on the Stiefel manifold, but they are often first-order, which may limit the efficiency under high-accuracy requirements. To this end, we propose a second-order method landing on the Stiefel manifold without invoking retractions, which is proved to enjoy local quadratic (or superlinear for its inexact variant) convergence. The update consists of the sum of (i) a component tangent to the level set of the constraint-defining function that aims to reduce the objective and (ii) a component normal to the same level set that reduces the infeasibility. Specifically, we construct the normal component via Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz, a fixed-point iteration for orthogonalization. Moreover, we establish a geometric connection between the Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz iteration and Stiefel manifolds, in which Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz moves along the normal space. For the tangent component, we formulate a modified Newton equation that incorporates Newton$\unicode{x2013}$Schulz. Numerical experiments on the orthogonal Procrustes problem, principal component analysis, and real-data independent component analysis illustrate that the proposed method performs better than the existing methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Agentopic: A Generative AI Agent Workflow for Explainable Topic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00833)
*Brice Valentin Kok-Shun,Johnny Chan,Gabrielle Peko,David Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agentopic 是一个基于多智能体的可解释主题建模工作流，利用大语言模型的推理能力，通过协作智能体进行主题识别、验证、分层和自然语言解释，在 BBC 数据集上达到 0.95 的 F1 分数，并生成了 2045 个跨六个层次的主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主题建模方法（如 LDA 和 BERTopic）在主题分配或分组的透明度上存在不足，缺乏可解释性，难以满足金融、医疗等关键领域对可追溯推理的需求。

Method: 提出 Agentopic 框架，采用多个 LLM 驱动的智能体协同完成主题识别、验证、层次化分组和自然语言解释，将可解释性嵌入整个工作流程。

Result: 在 BBC 数据集上，有种子引导时 F1 分数达 0.95（与 GPT-4.1 持平，优于 LDA 的 0.93，接近 BERTopic 的 0.98）；无种子时生成 2045 个语义连贯的主题，组织在六个层次结构中，远超原始的五类别结构。

Conclusion: Agentopic 通过嵌入可解释性的多智能体协作，为黑盒模型提供了可解释的替代方案，在金融和医疗等关键应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Agentopic is a novel agent-based workflow for explainable topic modeling that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing topic modeling approaches such as Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) and BERTopic often lack transparency on how topics are assigned or grouped. Agentopic addresses this by using multiple agents that collaboratively perform topic identification, validation, hierarchical grouping, and natural language explanation. This design enables users to trace the reasoning behind topic assignments, enhancing interpretability without sacrificing accuracy. When seeded with topics from the British Broadcasting Corporation (BBC) dataset, Agentopic achieves an F1-score of 0.95, matching GPT-4.1, improving on LDA (0.93), and close to BERTopic (0.98). We used Agentopic to augment the BBC dataset with generated explanations to improve the dataset's richness and context. The unseeded Agentopic generated 2045 semantically coherent topics organized across six hierarchical levels, vastly enriching the original five-category structure. By embedding explainability throughout the workflow, Agentopic offers an interpretable alternative to black-box models, making it particularly valuable for crucial applications like finance and healthcare.

</details>


### [209] [Polynomial-Time Optimal Group Selection via the Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00834)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明代数多样性框架中的群选择问题可简化为广义特征值问题，提出一种多项式时间算法（复杂度O(d²M²+d³)），通过双交换子矩阵的最小特征向量直接构造最优群生成器闭式解，且提供可认证的最优性间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多样性框架中群选择问题需要从M维观测数据的未知协方差结构中找出最优有限群，但枚举对称群S_M的所有子群是指数级复杂度的，亟需高效算法。

Method: 将组合群选择问题转化为广义特征值问题：从协方差矩阵的双交换子出发，构建双交换子矩阵，其最小特征向量直接给出最优群生成器的闭式解，算法复杂度O(d²M²+d³)，d为生成子基的维数。

Result: 双交换子最小特征值为零当且仅当最优生成子位于基的扩张中；非零时其幅值提供可认证的最优性间隙。该问题不属于Garey和Johnson标准复杂度分类，属于群论、矩阵分析与统计估计的新类别。

Conclusion: 双交换子公式是同时满足多项式时间、闭式解和可认证性的唯一方法，并与独立成分分析（JADE）、结构化矩阵逼近问题和同时矩阵对角化问题建立了联系。

Abstract: The algebraic diversity framework replaces temporal averaging over multiple observations with algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. The central open problem in this framework is $\textit{group selection}$: given an $M$-dimensional observation with unknown covariance structure, find the finite group whose spectral decomposition best matches the covariance. Naive enumeration of all subgroups of the symmetric group $S_M$ requires exponential time in $M$. We prove that this combinatorial problem reduces to a generalized eigenvalue problem derived from the double commutator of the covariance matrix, yielding a polynomial-time algorithm with complexity $O(d^2M^2 + d^3)$, where $d$ is the dimension of a generator basis. The minimum eigenvector of the double-commutator matrix directly constructs the optimal group generator in closed form, with no iterative optimization. The reduction is exact: the double-commutator minimum eigenvalue is zero if and only if the optimal generator lies in the span of the basis, and its magnitude provides a certifiable optimality gap when it does not. This problem does not appear in the standard catalogs of computational complexity (Garey and Johnson, 1979) and represents a new class linking group theory, matrix analysis, and statistical estimation. We establish connections to independent component analysis (JADE), structured matrix nearness problems, and simultaneous matrix diagonalization, and we show that the double-commutator formulation is the unique approach that is simultaneously polynomial-time, closed-form, and certifiable.

</details>


### [210] [Sparse Regression under Correlation and Weak Signals: A Reproducible Benchmark of Classical and Bayesian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00835)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在相关特征、弱信号和高维条件下，系统对比了6种稀疏回归方法（OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab），进行了超过2,600次实验。Bayesian方法（尤其是Horseshoe）在预测误差和覆盖概率上表现优异，但Spike-and-Slab存在欠覆盖问题；变量选择方面Lasso与Spike-and-Slab持平，Lasso在不需要后验时是实用默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典惩罚估计（如Lasso）速度快但无法提供不确定性估计，而Bayesian方法（如Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab）能产生完整后验但计算成本高昂。两类方法在真正使稀疏回归困难的条件下（相关特征、弱信号、高维）缺乏系统的头对头比较。

Method: 在合成数据上设置三种协方差结构（rho up to 0.9）、四个信噪比水平、p∈{20,50,100}，以及在Diabetes真实数据集上，对OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab六种方法进行超过2,600次实验的基准测试。

Result: Bayesian方法在预测误差上胜出（MSE 72 vs. 108-267），Horseshoe达到接近名义水平的95%覆盖（94.8%）；Spike-and-Slab区间更窄但欠覆盖（91.9%），可能与连续松弛有关；变量选择方面Lasso和Spike-and-Slab的F1分数持平（~0.47）。

Conclusion: Bayesian方法在预测和不确定性量化上优势明显，但Spike-and-Slab的覆盖问题需要关注；Lasso在变量选择上具有竞争力，当不需要后验分布时是实用默认选择。

Abstract: Choosing between classical and Bayesian sparse regression methods involves a real trade-off: penalized estimators like Lasso run in milliseconds but give no uncertainty estimates,while Horseshoe and Spike-and-Slab priors produce full posteriors but need MCMC chains that take minutes per fit.Surprisingly few studies compare these two families head-to-head under the conditions that actually make sparse regression hard -- correlated features, weak signals, and growing dimensionality. We benchmark six methods (OLS, Ridge,Lasso, Elastic Net, Horseshoe, Spike-and-Slab) on synthetic data with three covariance structures (rho up to 0.9), four SNR levels, and p in {20, 50, 100}, plus the Diabetes dataset,totalling over 2,600 experiments. The results are clear on some points and nuanced on others. Bayesian methods win on prediction error (MSE 72 vs. 108-267), and the Horseshoe delivers near-nominal 95% coverage (94.8%). But Spike-and-Slab,despite narrower intervals, under-covers at 91.9% -- its continuous relaxation likely plays a role. For variable selection, Lasso and Spike-and-Slab tie at F1 ~ 0.47, making Lasso the practical default when posteriors are not needed. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xiao98/sparse-bayesian-regression-bench.

</details>


### [211] [Fast Log-Domain Sinkhorn Optimal Transport with Warp-Level GPU Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00837)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FastSinkhorn，一种基于CUDA的对数域Sinkhorn算法原生实现，通过warp级shuffle归约和共享内存分块技术，在保证数值稳定性的同时实现高GPU利用率，在密集OT问题上相比POT库实现12倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sinkhorn算法实现存在两难：小正则化参数下数值不稳定，或深度学习框架带来显著开销，缺乏轻量且稳定的GPU求解器。

Method: 1) 完全在对数域计算，支持epsilon=1e-4的极小正则化参数；2) 采用warp级shuffle归约降低通信开销；3) 使用共享内存分块技术提高数据局部性和GPU利用率。

Result: 在n=m=8192的密集OT问题上，相比POT库实现12倍加速，相比PyTorch GPU基线实现5.9倍加速，仅消耗256 MB显存。

Conclusion: 原生CUDA核函数配合细致的数值处理，能为大规模最优传输计算提供高效且实用的基础。

Abstract: Entropic regularized optimal transport (OT) via the Sinkhorn algorithm has become a fundamental tool in machine learning, yet existing implementations either suffer from numerical instability for small regularization parameters or incur significant overhead from deep learning frameworks. We present FastSinkhorn, a lightweight, native CUDA implementation of the log-domain Sinkhorn algorithm that combines warp-level shuffle reductions with shared-memory tiling to achieve high GPU utilization without sacrificing numerical stability. Our solver operates entirely in the log-domain, enabling robust computation for regularization parameters as small as epsilon = 10^{-4} where standard-domain methods fail. On dense OT problems with n = m = 8192, our implementation achieves 12x speedup over the widely-used POT library and 5.9x speedup over GPU-accelerated PyTorch baselines, while consuming only 256 MB of GPU memory. We validate our solver on image color transfer, 3D point cloud matching, and convergence analysis, demonstrating that native CUDA kernels with careful numerical treatment provide a practical and efficient foundation for large-scale optimal transport computation.

</details>


### [212] [GAZE: Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation with Viewer-Level Tools and Literature Retrieval on Rare Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00876)
*Duaa Alim,Mogtaba Alim,Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAZE框架，让医学VLM通过迭代式工具调用（图像查看工具+医学检索工具）进行放射科报告生成，在NOVA基准上无需微调即实现病灶定位58.2 mAP@0.3和诊断准确率34.9%，且结构化提示本身就能提升性能，工具使用对罕见病帮助更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生通常反复检查图像并查阅文献后才撰写报告，而现有VLM仅通过单次前向传播生成文本，缺乏这种迭代式推理能力。本文旨在弥合这一差距，使医学VLM能够以类似放射科医生的方式工作。

Method: 提出GAZE框架，赋予医学VLM调用两类工具的能力：(1) 图像查看工具（缩放、窗宽窗位、对比度、边缘检测）；(2) 基于美国国家医学图书馆的检索工具（PubMed检索医学文献、Open-i检索放射影像）。输出通过结构化提示和模式验证确保格式规范，并记录完整工具调用轨迹以便审计。

Result: 在NOVA基准（906例脑MRI，281种罕见神经系统疾病）上：病灶定位58.2 mAP@0.3，Top-1诊断准确率34.9%。结构化提示从20.2提升至29.4 mAP@0.3。工具使用对罕见病帮助更大（IoU>0.3比例从17%升至58% vs 常见病25%升至68%）。检索消融实验揭示诊断与定位之间存在模型依赖的权衡。

Conclusion: GAZE框架通过迭代式工具调用有效提升了医学VLM在罕见病诊断和病灶定位上的性能，验证了框架设计本身作为实验变量的重要性，并强调了医学VLM需要联合评估诊断、定位和字幕生成三项任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) read an image and produce text in a single forward pass, whereas radiologists typically inspect an image several times and consult the literature before writing a report. We introduce GAZE (Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation), a framework that lets a medical VLM work in this iterative way by calling viewer-level tools (zoom, windowing, contrast, edge detection) and two retrieval tools backed by the U.S. National Library of Medicine (PubMed for medical literature, Open-i for radiological images), with structured outputs validated against a schema and full tool-call traces recorded for auditability. On NOVA, a benchmark of 906 brain MRI cases covering 281 rare neurological conditions, GAZE reaches 58.2 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union (IoU) 0.3 for lesion localisation and 34.9% Top-1 diagnostic accuracy under a joint protocol that scores captioning, diagnosis, and localisation from the image alone, without task-specific fine-tuning. Before any tool is used, structured prompting and schema-validated outputs already improve over the published Gemini 2.0 Flash baseline (20.2 to 29.4 mAP@0.3), so framework design is itself an experimental variable. Tool use helps rare pathologies disproportionately: the fraction of cases with IoU > 0.3 rises from 17% to 58% for diagnoses with three or fewer examples versus 25% to 68% for common conditions ($\geq$10 cases), with gains tracking engagement (Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d = 0.79, 11.8 tool calls per case; Gemini 2.0 Flash: tools used in 8.2% of cases, no significant benefit). Retrieval ablations additionally reveal a model-dependent trade-off in which gains in diagnosis can coincide with losses in localisation, reinforcing the case for joint evaluation of diagnosis, localisation, and captioning in medical VLMs.

</details>


### [213] [Linking spatial biology and clinical histology via Haiku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00925)
*Yan Cui,Jacob S. Leiby,Wenhui Lei,Dokyoon Kim,Yanxiang Deng,Aaron T. Mayer,Zhenqin Wu,Alexandro E. Trevino,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Haiku，一种基于三重模态对比学习的模型，整合了多重免疫荧光(mIF)、H&E组织学与临床元数据。模型在3218张组织切片(1606名患者，11种器官类型)上训练，实现了三模态交叉检索、下游分类与临床预测任务提升，并支持零样本生物标志物推断。在多项任务中优于基线方法，并引入反事实预测框架以揭示与癌症进展和生存结果相关的微环境分子变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础与转化生物医学研究需要整合分子、形态学和临床数据，但缺乏系统性框架来联合建模这些多模态数据。

Method: Haiku采用三重模态对比学习，在共享嵌入空间中对齐mIF空间蛋白质组学斑块、H&E组织学图像和临床元数据，支持三路交叉检索与反事实预测分析。

Result: 交叉检索Recall@50达0.611(基线接近零)；生存预测C-index 0.737(+7.91%相对提升)；52种生物标志物零样本推断平均Pearson相关0.718。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐通过Haiku实现了空间生物学的整合分析，弥合了分子测量与临床上下文之间的鸿沟，为生物探索提供了新途径。

Abstract: Integrating molecular, morphological, and clinical data is essential for basic and translational biomedical research, yet systematic frameworks for jointly modeling these modalities remain limited. Here we present Haiku, a tri-modal contrastive learning model trained on multiplexed immunofluorescence (mIF). It comprises 26.7 million spatial proteomics patches from 3,218 tissue sections across 1,606 patients spanning 11 organ types, with matched hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology and clinical metadata aligned in a shared embedding space. Haiku enables three-way cross-modal retrieval, improves downstream classification and clinical prediction tasks over unimodal baselines, and supports zero-shot biomarker inference through fusion retrieval conditioned on clinical metadata-only text descriptions. Across tasks, Haiku outperforms competing approaches, achieving cross-modal retrieval (Recall@50 up to 0.611 versus near-zero baseline), survival prediction (C-index 0.737, +7.91% relative improvement), and zero-shot biomarker inference (mean Pearson correlation 0.718 across 52 biomarkers). Furthermore, we introduce a counterfactual prediction framework in which modifying only clinical metadata while fixing tissue morphology surfaces niche-specific molecular shifts associated with breast cancer stage progression and lung cancer survival outcomes. In a lung adenocarcinoma case study, the counterfactual analysis recovers niche-specific shifts characterized by increased CD8 and granzyme B, reduced PD-L1, and decreased Ki67, broadly consistent with patterns reported for favorable outcomes. We present these counterfactual results as exploratory, hypothesis-generating signals rather than mechanistic claims. These capabilities demonstrate that tri-modal alignment via Haiku enables integrative analysis of spatial biology, bridging molecular measurements with clinical context for biological exploration.

</details>


### [214] [A Review of the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve and a Proof About the Area Beneath It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00926)
*Steven Redolfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的概率解释（即AUC等于随机正样本排在随机负样本之上的概率），给出了当假设不满足时该解释偏离真实值的界限，并对ROC曲线相关文献进行了小型综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROC曲线及其下面积（AUC）被广泛用于衡量二分类器性能，其概率解释（AUC等于随机正样本排在随机负样本之上的概率）被广泛引用，但该解释成立的条件及不满足假设时的误差界限尚未被严格形式化。

Method: 通过数学形式化证明AUC的概率解释，推导当模型假设不满足时该解释的误差上界，并辅以文献综述梳理相关研究。

Result: 严格形式化了AUC的概率解释，并给出了当假设条件不成立时该解释偏离真实值的定量界限。

Conclusion: AUC的概率解释在特定假设下成立，当假设不满足时存在可量化的偏差，研究者在使用该解释时需注意其适用条件。

Abstract: The Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve of a binary classifier has often been utilized to measure the performance of the classifier. The area beneath this curve is used in particular because of its quoted probabilistic interpretation as being equal to the probability that the classifier will rank a random positive observation above a random negative observation. This paper formalizes this claim, produces a bound on how far away from the truth it is if a hypothesis is not met, and gives a small literature review of the ROC curve.

</details>


### [215] [CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Zechen Li,Flora Salim,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGM-JEPA和X-CGM-JEPA两个自监督预训练框架，通过掩码潜在表示预测实现跨模态、跨场景的连续血糖监测表征学习，在三个评估场景下均取得最优或次优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM数据存在多视角问题（时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity摘要），单视角表征在模态或场景迁移时表现不佳，且基线模型在不同迁移场景下性能不稳定。

Method: CGM-JEPA采用自监督预训练框架，预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值以获得抽象表征；X-CGM-JEPA在此基础上增加掩码Glucodensity跨视角目标，融合分布信息。在228名受试者约38.9万条CGM数据上预训练，并在两个临床队列三个评估场景下验证。

Result: X-CGM-JEPA在三个场景的两个指标上均排名第一或第二，在队列泛化场景超出最强基线6.5个百分点，静脉到CGM迁移场景超出3.6个百分点（p<0.001）；模态迁移时种族AUROC差距缩小25-54%；稀疏域内静脉数据上ARI提升39%，NMI提升40%。

Conclusion: 跨视图的潜在表示抽象能有效解决CGM多模态迁移和基线不一致问题，且对弱势子群公平性有所改善。

Abstract: Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) can detect early metabolic subphenotypes (insulin resistance, IR; $β$-cell dysfunction), but population-scale deployment faces two coupled problems. First, the same physiological state appears through multiple views (CGM time series, venous OGTT, Glucodensity summaries), so single-view representations fail to transfer when deployment shifts the modality or setting. Second, baselines perform inconsistently across these shifts. Both problems point to one remedy: representations that abstract away from any single view to capture higher-level temporal and distributional structure. We propose CGM-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework which predicts masked latent representations rather than raw values, yielding abstraction that transfers across modalities. X-CGM-JEPA adds a masked Glucodensity cross-view objective for complementary distributional information. We pretrain on $\sim$389k unlabeled CGM readings from 228 subjects and evaluate on two clinical cohorts ($N=27$ and $N=17$ public-release subsets) across three regimes (cohort generalization, venous-to-CGM transfer, home CGM) under 20-iteration $\times$ 2-fold cross-validation. X-CGM-JEPA ranks first or second on AUROC for both endpoints across all three regimes while no baseline does, exceeding the strongest baseline by up to $+6.5$ pp in cohort generalization and $+3.6$ pp in venous-to-CGM transfer (paired Wilcoxon, $p<0.001$). Under modality shift, it matches mean AUROC while redistributing toward weaker subgroups (ethnicity AUROC gap shrinks 25-54%); on sparse in-domain venous data, the distributional view lifts label-aware clustering (ARI $+39\%$, NMI $+40\%$). Code and weights: https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA

</details>


### [216] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytic-CPD，一种基于结构化解析映射的非刚体点集配准方法，用有限维解析映射替代传统CPD中的高斯核位移场，通过重心凝聚将软对应转化为加权解析拟合问题，并引入度数延续策略稳定大变形配准。实验表明在二维解析变形和三维非解析变形上均优于标准CPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPD使用基于高斯核的位移场，其变形参数规模受限于点集大小（M×M核矩阵），导致计算复杂且缺乏可解释性。本文旨在用结构化解析映射替代核方法，实现更紧凑、可解释且高效的非刚体配准。

Method: 1) 保留CPD的后验对应层（高斯混合模型）；2) 将点索引的高斯核位移场M步替换为有限维结构化解析映射估计器；3) 通过重心恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点，将软对应目标转化为加权解析拟合；4) 用截断多元泰勒映射表示变形，参数维度仅由空间维度和解析阶数控制；5) 引入度数延续策略逐步激活高阶解析模式以稳定大变形配准。

Result: 在二维解析变形和三维光滑非解析变形实验中，Analytic-CPD在代表性大变形场景下比标准CPD实现了更低的最终误差和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CPD风格的概率对应与结构化解析映射的结合，为基于核的非刚体配准提供了一种紧凑且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [217] [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935)
*An Huang,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)机制，揭示扩散模型中的时间步嵌入可被用作侧信道进行恶意信息注入，并进行了理论分析与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中时间步嵌入组件在安全风险和可靠溯源方面的研究存在空白，尽管其包含大量信息但未被充分探索。

Method: 通过分析时间步嵌入空间的表征能力，发现不同时间步可编码侧信道信息；利用调度器接口实现攻击与防御；从位置编码映射角度进行理论分析，并引入互相干性评估解释时间步区间的可分离性。

Result: 揭示了扩散模型时间步可作为强大的侧信道携带专用信息，证明了时间步嵌入空间可用于恶意信息注入。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解扩散模型的时间维度提供了新的方向，推动了对抗性生成建模的新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the foundation of modern generative systems, with most research focusing primarily on improving generation efficiency and output quality. The timestep embedding component is a crucial part of the diffusion pipeline, which provides a temporal conditioning signal to the denoising network, enabling it to adapt its predictions across different noise levels throughout the process. Despite their potential to contain substantial information, timestep embeddings remain underexplored in current research, especially for security risks and reliable provenance. To fill this gap, we introduce Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE), a novel mechanism that investigates the underutilized temporal space for malicious information injection into diffusion models. In particular, when zooming in on the timestep embedding space, we find that different timesteps exhibit distinct representational capabilities that can encode side-channel information. Moreover, such encoded information can be utilized for attack and defense purposes through the scheduler interface. We present a theoretical analysis of timestep embeddings as position-encoding mappings and derive a mutual coherence evaluation that explains the separability of disjoint timestep intervals. Our findings reveal the diffusion model's timestep as a powerful side channel for carrying dedicated information, motivating new directions for adversarial generative modeling by understanding the temporal dimension.

</details>


### [218] [EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936)
*Luan Pham,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds,Hui Guan,Daniel Kroening*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventADL是首个面向云服务系统的开箱式基于事件的异常检测与定位框架，通过学习事件语义模式（ESP）和事件频率模式（EFP）来检测异常，并利用干预图进行根因定位。在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实事件上，异常检测F1≥90%，根因定位Top-3准确率达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测与定位方法主要关注指标和日志数据，忽略了事件数据这一重要数据源。本文基于520个真实事件系统分析，发现事件数据中蕴含着异常及其根因的表现模式，因此需要设计专门针对事件数据的ADL框架。

Method: EventADL包含三个阶段：(1)离线训练阶段，先学习事件语义模式（ESP）捕捉正常交互关系，再学习事件频率模式（EFP）捕捉已知ESP的正常频率；(2)在线检测阶段，检测与任一模式显著偏离的事件流数据；(3)根因定位阶段，构建干预图建模系统交互与异常关系，自动定位根因。框架支持无标签数据，产出可解释的异常及根因。

Result: 在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实事件上的评估表明，EventADL异常检测F1≥90%，根因定位Top-3准确率达100%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: EventADL有效填补了基于事件的ADL空白，通过ESP和EFP双模式检测以及干预图定位，实现了高效、可解释的异常检测与根因定位，且无需人工标注数据。

Abstract: Anomaly detection and localization (ADL) is critical for maintaining reliability and availability in cloud systems. Recent ADL developments focus on metric and log data, leaving event data unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EventADL, the first open-box event-based ADL framework for cloud-based service systems. To motivate the design of our framework, we conduct a systematic analysis on 520 real-world incidents, and provide insights into how anomalies and their root causes manifest through event data. EventADL has three phases: offline training, online anomaly detection, and root cause localization. During the training phase, EventADL first learns Event Semantic Patterns (ESPs), which capture normal interactions between system entities using historical event data, and then learns Event Frequency Patterns (EFPs), which capture the normal frequency of known ESPs. In the online anomaly detection phase, any data in the event stream that deviates significantly from either pattern is identified as anomalous. For localization, EventADL constructs an Intervention Graph that models the relationships between recent system interactions and the detected anomalies for automatic root cause localization. The framework is designed to operate efficiently with unlabeled data and to produce interpretable anomalies with their corresponding root causes. Our evaluation on three real cloud service systems and two real-world incidents demonstrates that EventADL outperforms existing methods, achieving F1-scores of at least 90% for anomaly detection and 100% top-3 accuracy in root cause localization.

</details>


### [219] [Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938)
*Bin Chen,Zhuoya Meng,Fang Yang,Runkang Guo,Jingtao Ding,Yin Zhang,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEDAN模型，一种基于结构增强扩散机制的属性图模型，用于生成泛化性强的城市通勤OD矩阵，通过融合区域语义属性（人口、POI特征）与空间结构（邻接矩阵、距离矩阵），在真实数据集上相比最优基线WEDAN提升7.38%的RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体意图、地理约束和社会动态的共同影响导致通勤模式呈现显著异质性，使得现有生成模型难以跨城市泛化。

Method: 将城市建模为属性图，区域为节点（含人口和POI特征），通勤流为加权边；设计融合机制联合建模语义和空间信息：图-Transformer节点交互建模潜在出行需求，邻接矩阵引导注意力权重增强邻居区域交互，距离矩阵作为扩散条件捕捉空间邻近性和旅行阻抗。

Result: 在美国多个城市真实OD数据集上，SEDAN相比最优基线WEDAN在RMSE上提升7.38%，并在不同城市场景和结构模式下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SEDAN通过融合城市语义和空间约束，能够生成行为合理且地理一致的OD矩阵，为通勤OD矩阵生成提供了一种有效且泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of commuting flows is important for urban governance, traffic planning, and resource allocation. However, the combined influence of individual intentions, geographic constraints, and social dynamics leads to considerable heterogeneity in commuting patterns, making it difficult to develop generation models that generalize across cities. To address this issue, we propose SEDAN, a Structure-Enhanced Diffusion model conditioned on Attributed Nodes for generalizable OD matrix generation. SEDAN models a city as an attributed graph. Each region is treated as a node with demographic and point-of-interest features, and commuting flows are modeled as weighted edges. Adjacency and distance matrices are incorporated to characterize spatial structure. Based on this representation, we design a fusion mechanism within SEDAN to jointly model semantic information and spatial information. Regional semantic attributes are used to model latent travel demand through graph-transformer-based node interactions, while spatial structure is injected into the generation process as explicit constraints. The adjacency matrix guides attention weights to strengthen interactions between neighboring regions. Meanwhile, the distance matrix serves as a diffusion condition to capture spatial proximity and travel impedance. The fusion of urban semantics and spatial constraints enables SEDAN to generate OD matrices that are both behaviorally plausible and geographically coherent. Experiments on real-world OD datasets from U.S. cities show that SEDAN achieves a 7.38\% improvement in RMSE over the state-of-the-art baseline, WEDAN. It also remains robust across heterogeneous urban scenarios and varying structural patterns. Our work provides an effective and generalizable solution for commuting OD matrix generation. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SEDAN.

</details>


### [220] [Divergence is Uncertainty: A Closed-Form Posterior Covariance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00941)
*Jiarui Xing,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于任何预训练的流匹配模型，后验协方差的迹等于速度场的散度（乘以已知时间因子再加常数），称为散度-不确定性恒等式。该恒等式无需重新训练或修改架构，可精确计算生成不确定性，在MNIST上验证了语义有效性和计算效率（比集成方法快约10000倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成框架中样本不确定性的量化仍是一个开放问题，现有方法要么需要重新训练辅助头、维护昂贵的集成模型，要么通过多步积分传播近似协方差，在训练成本、推理成本和精度之间进行权衡。

Method: 理论证明了后验协方差迹与速度场散度之间的闭合形式恒等式（称为散度-不确定性恒等式），以及矩阵级形式依赖于速度雅可比矩阵。该恒等式是精确的、事后可计算的，适用于任何预训练的流匹配模型。

Result: 在MNIST上，生成的逐像素不确定性图语义上有意义（集中在数字边界等高变异性区域），标量不确定性分数能跟踪实际预测误差，且计算量比集成方法和蒙特卡洛dropout低约10000倍。

Conclusion: 该恒等式表明，在不牺牲训练成本、推理成本或精度的情况下，可以精确量化流匹配模型的不确定性，为生成模型的不确定性估计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Flow matching has become a leading framework for generative modeling, but quantifying the uncertainty of its samples remains an open problem. Existing approaches retrain the model with auxiliary variance heads, maintain costly ensembles, or propagate approximate covariance through many integration steps, trading off training cost, inference cost, or accuracy. We show that none of these trade-offs is necessary. We prove that, for any pre-trained flow matching velocity field, the trace of the posterior covariance over the clean data given the current state equals, in closed form, the divergence of the velocity field, up to a known time-dependent prefactor and an additive constant. We call this the \emph{divergence-uncertainty identity} for flow matching. The matrix-level form of the identity is similarly closed-form, depending solely on the velocity Jacobian. Because the identity is exact and post-hoc, it is computable on any pre-trained flow matching model, with no retraining and no architectural modification. For one-step generators such as MeanFlow, the same identity yields the exact end-to-end generation uncertainty in a single forward pass, eliminating the multi-step variance propagation required by all prior methods. Experiments on MNIST confirm that the resulting per-pixel uncertainty maps are semantically meaningful, concentrating on digit boundaries where inter-sample variation is highest, and that the scalar uncertainty score tracks actual prediction error, all at roughly 10,000$\times$ less total compute than ensembling or Monte Carlo dropout.

</details>


### [221] [Finite-Sample Analysis of Elimination in Active Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01039)
*Ziyuan Lin,Hoang Ngoc Nguyen,Jie Xu,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带假设消除的主动假设检验问题，提出消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，通过渐进消除非主导假设并重新分配感知资源，在有限样本下获得更优的停止时间上界，实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多安全关键应用中，需要在有限样本下以固定置信度进行主动假设检验，现有方法在停止时间上仍有优化空间，特别是如何通过假设消除来加速决策过程。

Method: 提出消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，包含两个关键设计：(1) 冠军专属的主动对手集渐进剪枝，(2) 感知资源向存活的备择假设重新分配。引入激进度参数来调节消除速度与置信保证之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出期望停止时间的非渐近上界，证明消除带来的有限样本增益体现在非主导项的量级上，源于缩减假设集后更紧的跟踪和集中常数。合成高斯数据的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 假设消除能够有效提升主动假设检验的有限样本效率，其增益主要体现在非渐近项上，激进度参数提供了灵活的速度-置信度权衡机制。

Abstract: A fixed-confidence, finite-sample problem of active hypothesis testing arises in many safety-critical applications. Situated in the context of sequential hypothesis testing, this paper studies the effect of hypothesis elimination on the stopping time. We introduce an elimination-augmented Track-and-Stop algorithm, in which champion-specific active-opponent sets are progressively pruned, and sensing effort is reallocated toward the surviving alternatives. Our analysis derives a non-asymptotic upper bound on the expected stopping time. The gain in finite-sample from elimination appears on the scale of the non-leading term, resulting from tighter tracking and concentration constants on the reduced hypothesis set. Furthermore, we introduce an aggressiveness parameter to modulate the trade-off between faster elimination and weaker confidence guarantee. An experimental study on synthetic Gaussian instances confirms the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [222] [Learning in the Fisher Subspace: A Guided Initialization for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01046)
*Zhi-Quan Feng,Ying-Jia Lin,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Fisher信息的数据感知LoRA初始化方法，通过下游数据曲率信息选择适配子空间，相比传统基于权重几何的初始化策略显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化仅依赖预训练权重的内在几何特性（如权重幅值），忽略了模型与下游数据分布的交互关系，导致初始化分配容量到任务无关方向，严重限制下游性能。

Method: 将LoRA初始化形式化为在目标数据分布下识别参数空间中方向的影响程度，提出Fisher引导框架，利用下游数据诱导的曲率信息（Fisher信息矩阵）刻画参数扰动对模型预测的影响，从而选择与目标任务对齐的LoRA适配方向。

Result: 在多种任务和模态上的实验结果表明，数据感知的初始化方法相比现有方法持续且显著地提升了下游性能。

Conclusion: LoRA初始化应由数据感知的敏感性（而非仅权重幅值）来指导，Fisher信息提供了 principled 的、任务依赖的方向选择准则，使适配更好地与目标对齐。

Abstract: LoRA adapts large language models (LLMs) by restricting updates to low-rank subspaces of pre-trained weights. While this substantially reduces training cost, the effectiveness of adaptation critically depends on which subspace is chosen at initialization: a poor initialization that allocates capacity to task-irrelevant directions can severely hinder downstream performance. Existing initialization strategies primarily rely on the intrinsic properties of pre-trained weights, implicitly assuming that weight geometry alone reflects task relevance. However, such criteria overlook how the model interacts with the downstream data distribution.
