In this work, we formulate LoRA initialization as identifying the degree of impact of directions in parameter space under the target data distribution. We argue that data-aware sensitivity, rather than weight-only magnitude, should govern the choice of adaptation subspaces. Building on this perspective, we propose a Fisher-guided framework that leverages curvature information induced by downstream data to characterize how parameter perturbations influence model predictions. This perspective yields a principled, task-dependent criterion for selecting LoRA directions that better align adaptation with the target objective. Empirical results across diverse tasks and modalities demonstrate that data-aware initialization consistently and significantly improves downstream performance over existing approaches.

</details>


### [223] [LEAP: Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01058)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Naryan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Haoan Wang,Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Rishi Bhatia,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现层级对齐蒸馏与基于收敛的早退机制存在系统不兼容性，并提出了LEAP（逐层退出感知预训练）辅助训练目标来调和这一矛盾。LEAP-MiniLM在保持高准确率的同时实现了1.61倍实测加速，91.9%样本在7层内退出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法使中间层表征趋近教师模型，抑制了早退机制依赖的表征收敛性，导致两者在标准部署条件下系统不兼容。

Method: 提出LEAP辅助训练目标，在标准蒸馏基础上增加约束条件，要求中间层逼近最终层表征，无需修改模型架构。

Result: LEAP-MiniLM在batch=1、NVIDIA L4上实现1.61倍实测墙钟加速（θ=0.95），91.9%样本在7层内退出，1.80倍理论层数缩减，而标准蒸馏模型有效加速为零。STS-B上达到0.760±0.006，BEIR上也有验证。

Conclusion: LEAP有效调和了层级对齐蒸馏与早退机制的不兼容性，为蒸馏模型的推理加速提供了实用方案，并给出了延迟测量、决策阈值和部署标准等操作指导。

Abstract: Layer-aligned distillation and convergence-based early exit represent two predominant computational efficiency paradigms for transformer inference; yet we establish that they exhibit systematic incompatibility under standard deployment conditions for convergence-based early exit. Distillation objectives that align intermediate student layers to teacher representations suppress the representational convergence that early-exit mechanisms exploit, rendering such mechanisms ineffective on distilled models. We introduce LEAP (Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining), an auxiliary training objective that reconciles this incompatibility. LEAP requires no architectural modifications; it augments standard distillation with a single constraint ensuring intermediate layers approximate final-layer representations. LEAP-MiniLM achieves 1.61$\times$ measured wall-clock speedup (batch=1, NVIDIA L4) at $θ$=0.95, with 91.9% of samples exiting by layer 7 and 1.80$\times$ theoretical layer reduction, where standard distilled models achieve zero effective speedup. We validate across sentence similarity (STS-B: 0.760 $\pm$ 0.006) and retrieval benchmarks (BEIR), providing operational guidance including latency measurements, decision thresholds, and deployment criteria.

</details>


### [224] [GEODE: Angle-Adaptive OOD Detection with Universal Scorer Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01063)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEODE方法，通过保几何结构的合成边界校准（角度自适应范数损失）来复现Outlier Exposure的OOD检测效果，无需辅助数据，在所有七种评分器上均表现优异且无灾难性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Outlier Exposure在OOD检测中表现强劲但存在评分器依赖的权衡（如MSP强、KNN弱），且需要精心筛选的辅助数据。作者希望揭示OE有效的原因，并设计一种无需辅助数据、对所有评分器均有效的OOD检测方法。

Method: GEODE方法：提出角度自适应范数损失（angle-adaptive norm loss），其中每个样本的目标范数根据其与最近类中心的余弦相似度进行缩放，从而在边界结构重要的区域保持特征几何结构。该方法基于神经坍缩（neural collapse）的四个定理进行理论支撑。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，GEODE在所有七种评分器上的近OOD AUROC达89.0-92.3，远OOD达93.05，无任何评分器出现灾难性失效。与OE结合后，CIFAR-10上MSP达95.0、KNN达94.8；在CIFAR-100上所有评分器均超越OE。在WRN-28-10上平均提升+4.5 Energy。对比方法PFS（KNN AUROC仅14.38，低于随机）存在严重几何破坏问题。

Conclusion: OE的本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖；GEODE通过保几何结构的合成校准实现了对OE的替代，同时避免了OE对辅助数据的依赖和评分器间的性能权衡，为实际部署中OOD类型未知的场景提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Outlier Exposure (OE) is among the strongest training-based OOD detectors on standard benchmarks but exhibits scorer-dependent tradeoffs (e.g., strong on MSP, weak on KNN) and requires curated auxiliary data. We show why OE works: its features sit at the same geometric locus as real near-OOD data, with the boundary-adjacent quartile driving nearly all of OE's gain. OE is boundary calibration, not OOD coverage. GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection) replicates this calibration synthetically through an angle-adaptive norm loss in which targets scale per-sample with cosine similarity to the nearest class mean, preserving feature geometry where boundary structure matters. Four theorems grounded in neural collapse justify the design. GEODE works across all seven standard scorers on CIFAR-10 (near-OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3, far-OOD reaching 93.05; no catastrophic failure on any scorer). Since the OOD regime is unknown at deployment, this is the test that matters. GEODE outperforms vanilla CE at matched epoch counts. Combined with OE, GEODE reaches 95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN on CIFAR-10 and beats OE on every scorer on CIFAR-100. The gains hold on WRN-28-10 (+4.5 Energy, 3 seeds). Unlike methods that push OOD into the classifier null space (e.g., PFS, 14.38 KNN AUROC, worse than random), GEODE's adaptive target preserves the geometry that distance-based scorers depend on.

</details>


### [225] [A dimensional R2 regression metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01066)
*Jaesung Yoo,Stefan Lemke,Jian Zhong Guo,Kanaka Rajan,Adam Hantman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dim-R2指标，是对传统R2分数的扩展，克服了R2仅支持二维输入、单标量输出无法揭示预测精度模式、以及对低方差噪声敏感导致负值不可解释等三个关键局限性。Dim-R2可处理任意维度数据、提供多维精度视图、降低噪声敏感性，在合成数据和三维回归数据集上展示了优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R2分数存在三个关键局限：仅支持最多二维输入；将评估结果压缩为单一标量，隐藏了预测精度的丰富模式；对低方差噪声通道敏感，可能产生大量不可解释的负值。因此需要一种更灵活、可解释的回归评估指标。

Method: 提出Dim-R2分数，作为R2的简单扩展，接受任意维度数据，提供多维精度视图，并降低对噪声的敏感性。

Result: 在合成正弦数据以及三个多维回归数据集上验证了Dim-R2的优势。

Conclusion: Dim-R2作为一种可解释且灵活的回归评估指标，能够揭示回归精度的模式，指导回归建模。

Abstract: R2 score is the standard metric for evaluating regression tasks, offering a normalized magnitude-agnostic measure of accuracy that captures variance. However, R2 has three key limitations: it is limited to at most two dimensional inputs, it reduces the score to a single scalar that hides rich patterns of prediction accuracy, and it is sensitive to low-variance noise channels which can yield large, uninterpretable negative values. We introduce the Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2), a simple extension of R2 that accepts data of arbitrary dimensionality, provides a multidimensional view of accuracy, and reduces sensitivity to noise. We demonstrate its advantages on both synthetic sinusoidal data and three multidimensional regression datasets. Dim-R2 offers an interpretable and flexible metric that highlights patterns in regression accuracy, guiding regression modeling.

</details>


### [226] [Learning Discriminators for Resampling in the Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter through a Normalizing Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01089)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于判别器的重采样方法（discriminator-informed resampling），用于改进集合高斯混合滤波（EnGMF）。该方法通过归一化流学习判别器，在重采样阶段拒绝物理上不合理的粒子，从而在低集合规模下显著降低Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上的滤波误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EnGMF的重采样步骤可能生成物理上不真实的后验样本，进而导致无意义的预测。作者希望引入物理合理性约束来改进重采样过程。

Method: 提出判别器引导的重采样过程（discriminator-informed resampling），利用归一化流（normalizing flow）学习一个判别器，在重采样阶段对候选粒子进行接受/拒绝判断，仅保留物理上合理的粒子。

Result: 在Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上的数值实验表明，在低集合规模（low-ensemble regimes）下，所提方法相比标准EnGMF持续降低了滤波误差。

Conclusion: 判别器引导的重采样能有效提升EnGMF在低集合规模下的滤波精度，为粒子滤波中的物理一致性约束提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ensemble Gaussian mixture filter (EnGMF) is a powerful, convergent particle filter capable of medium-to-high dimensional non-linear filtering. The EnGMF relies on a resampling step that can generate physically unrealistic posterior samples, that would subsequently produce physically meaningless forecasts. This work introduces the discriminator-informed resampling procedure, that augments the posterior resampling step with a discriminator that accepts or rejects candidate particles based on their physical plausibility. In this work these discriminators are learned through a normalizing flow approach. Numerical experiments on both the Ikeda map and the Lorenz '63 system show that discriminator informed resampling procedure consistently reduces error relative to the standard EnGMF in low-ensemble regimes.

</details>


### [227] [Learning to Race in Minutes: Infoprop Dyna on the Mini Wheelbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01096)
*Devdutt Subhasish,Henrik Hose,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了Infoprop Dyna这一基于模型的不确定性感知强化学习框架，使欠驱动独轮机器人Mini Wheelbot仅通过11分钟的真实世界交互便学会在赛道上竞速，无需使用物理仿真器或域随机化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖精心设计的物理仿真器和域随机化才能实现有效的仿真到现实迁移，作者希望绕过这一限制，使机器人直接从真实世界交互中学习。

Method: 采用Infoprop Dyna，一种先进的不确定性感知的基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）框架，使机器人直接在真实环境中进行学习和交互。

Result: 欠驱动独轮机器人Mini Wheelbot在11分钟的真实世界交互后学会了在赛道上竞速。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的MBRL框架可以有效绕过对物理仿真器的依赖，实现快速、高效的机器人真实世界学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to enable robots with fast, nonlinear, and unstable dynamics to reach the limits of their performance. However, most recent advances rely on carefully designed physics-based simulators and domain randomization to achieve successful sim-to-real transfer within reasonable wall-clock time. In this work, we bypass the need for such simulators and demonstrate that Infoprop Dyna, a state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) framework, can enable robots to learn directly from real-world interactions. Using Infoprop Dyna, the Mini Wheelbot, an underactuated unicycle robot, learns to race around a track within 11 minutes of real-world experience.

</details>


### [228] [When Less is Enough: Efficient Inference via Collaborative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01111)
*Yilei Chen,Sharut Gupta,Yannis Paschalidis,Ayush Sekhari,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUET提出了一种双模型高效两阶段推理框架，由能力强的模型生成推理信号，轻量级模型解释信号并生成最终答案，通过长度惩罚联合训练目标，在保持推理性能的同时节省高达60%的大模型输出token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一大型模型进行端到端推理和预测会产生高昂的推理成本，需要降低大模型的推理开销同时保持任务性能。

Method: 提出DUET两阶段协作推理框架：能力强的模型负责生成推理信号，轻量级模型负责解释信号并生成最终答案；采用长度惩罚联合训练目标，鼓励能力强的模型仅传输对轻量级模型完成任务足够的信息。

Result: 在AIME和GPQA等具有挑战性的推理基准上，相比单独使用大模型进行端到端推理，DUET节省了高达60%的大模型输出token，同时保持强推理性能。

Conclusion: DUET通过将推理密集型计算分配给能力强的模型，非推理密集型部分委托给轻量级模型，实现了在显著降低推理成本的同时不牺牲任务性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DUET (Dual-model Efficient Two-stage inference), a collaborative inference framework in which a capable model and a lightweight model work together to solve a task. Relying on a single large model to perform end-to-end reasoning and prediction often incurs substantial inference cost. In contrast, DUET decomposes inference into two stages: the capable model produces a reasoning signal, and the lightweight model interprets this signal to generate the final answer, allowing reasoning-intensive computation to be handled by the capable model while non-reasoning-intensive components are delegated to the lightweight model without sacrificing task performance. To achieve this objective, we propose a length-penalized joint training objective that encourages the capable model to transmit only the information that is sufficient for the lightweight model to solve the task. As a result, DUET maintains strong reasoning performance with substantially lower inference cost than end-to-end inference using a large model alone, saving up to 60% of the large model's output tokens on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including AIME and GPQA.

</details>


### [229] [Machine Learning-Augmented Acceleration of Iterative Ptychographic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01122)
*Bowen Zheng,Katayun Kamdin,David Shapiro,Alexander Ditter,Dayne Sasaki,Emma Bernard,Roopali Kukreja,Petrus H. Zwart,Slavomír Nemšák,Apurva Mehta,Nicholas Schwarz,Alexander Hexemer,Tanny Chavez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习增强的迭代叠层衍射成像重建方法，通过引入学习型前馈算子加速收敛，在保持物理一致性的同时将收敛速度提升两倍以上，并已成功部署于同步辐射光束线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代式叠层衍射成像重建算法在实际实验条件下收敛缓慢，影响实时实验操作效率。

Method: 在标准迭代初始化（热身）后，引入一个经机器学习训练的前馈算子将重建状态快速推进至更收敛的阶段，然后恢复常规迭代更新。模型在多样化的叠层衍射数据集上训练，并在不同年份采集的实验数据上评估。

Result: 相比传统迭代求解器，该方法在保持相当重建质量的同时，泊松负对数似然收敛更快，实际耗时降低两倍以上，并在不同年份的实验数据上展现出鲁棒性和时间泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法已集成到现有重建流水线并部署在同步辐射光束线的生产环境中，证明了其在实时实验操作中的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Iterative ptychographic reconstruction algorithms are widely used for coherent diffractive imaging but can exhibit slow convergence under realistic experimental conditions. We propose a machine learning-augmented approach that accelerates iterative ptychographic reconstruction by introducing a learned fast-forward operator applied during reconstruction. Following an initial warm-up using standard iterations, the fast-forward operator advances the reconstruction toward a more converged state, after which conventional iterative updates are resumed. This strategy preserves the physical consistency and flexibility of established ptychographic solvers while reducing the number of iterations required for convergence. The model is trained on diverse ptychographic datasets and evaluated on experimental data acquired in a different year, demonstrating robustness and temporal generalization. Compared with conventional iterative solvers, the machine learning-augmented method achieves comparable reconstruction quality while converging faster in terms of Poisson negative log-likelihood, yielding over a two-fold reduction in wall-clock time. The approach has been integrated into an existing reconstruction pipeline and deployed in production at a synchrotron beamline, demonstrating practicality for real-time experimental operation.

</details>


### [230] [Extreme Weather Bench: A framework and benchmark for evaluation of high-impact weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01126)
*Amy McGovern,Taylor Mandelbaum,Daniel Rothenberg,Nicholas Loveday,Corey Potvin,Montgomery Flora,Linus Magnusson,Eric Gilleland,John Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 Extreme Weather Bench (EWB) 的社区驱动开源基准测试套件，旨在通过标准化的高影响天气案例研究、观测数据、影响指标和开源代码，促进 AI 和数值天气预报模型在高影响天气事件上的验证与评估，提升模型的可信度并推动天气模型科学进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 天气模型评估方式存在不足：要么仅进行全球尺度评估，要么手工挑选少量案例或特定区域，缺乏标准化、开源且聚焦高影响天气的基准测试套件，难以进行公平的跨模型比较和针对高影响天气事件的系统性验证。

Method: EWB 提供了一套标准化案例研究（涵盖多时空尺度和不同天气谱系）、观测数据、基于影响的指标以及开源代码，支持用户对其模型进行验证。

Result: EWB 作为社区驱动的开源基准测试套件，能够促进不同天气模型间的真正对比，并在特定高影响天气现象上通过案例研究进行模型评估，从而提升 AI 模型的可信度。

Conclusion: EWB 将通过与全球天气与预报验证社区的合作，持续演进以纳入更多现象、测试案例和指标，助力推动所有天气模型的科学进步。

Abstract: Forecasting the wide variety of high-impact weather events experienced globally is a challenge for both Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) models and it is critical that such models be properly verified before deployment. Although AI weather models are rapidly evolving, much of their evaluation is currently done either with a global-scale evaluation or by hand-picking a small number of case studies or a region. A widely-used open-source benchmark suite focusing on high-impact weather will help to drive the science forward for all scales of weather models, as it has for other AI fields. Here we introduce Extreme Weather Bench (EWB), a new community-driven benchmark suite that facilitates model validation and verification on a variety of high-impact hazards that matter to people around the globe. EWB provides a standard set of case studies (spanning across multiple spatial and temporal scales and different parts of the weather spectrum), observational data, impact-based metrics, and open-source code for users to evaluate their models. Verifying that a model works against a standard set of case studies, especially events that are high-impact for the general public, is a key piece of improving the trustworthiness of AI models. EWB will help to drive the science forward for all weather models, enabling true comparisons across models and evaluating models on specific high-impact phenomena through the use of case studies. EWB is a free open-source community-driven system and will continue to evolve to include additional phenomena, test cases and metrics in collaboration with the worldwide weather and forecast verification community.

</details>


### [231] [Forager: a lightweight testbed for continual learning with partial observability in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01131)
*Steven Tang,Xinze Xiong,Anna Hakhverdyan,Andrew Patterson,Jacob Adkins,Jiamin He,Esraa Elelimy,Parham Mohammad Panahi,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Forager，一个轻量级、恒定内存占用的部分可观测持续强化学习环境，用于研究持续强化学习中的塑性损失和状态构建问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习实验多关注完全可观测MDP中的塑性损失问题，忽视了部分可观测性的作用，且部分可观测CRL环境通常计算成本过高，缺乏一个轻量级的环境来深入研究这些问题。

Method: 设计了Forager环境——一个轻量级、部分可观测的持续强化学习环境，具有恒定内存占用；提供了一系列实验和示例任务来测试CRL智能体的性能。

Result: Forager对现有CRL智能体具有挑战性；智能体表现出塑性损失；提出的缓解措施有一定效果；利用状态构建（state construction）最为有效。

Conclusion: Forager能有效揭示当前CRL智能体的局限性，状态构建是缓解塑性损失的最有效方法，且通过无限新任务变体进一步凸显了现有方法的不足。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning (CRL), good performance requires never-ending learning, acting, and exploration in a big, partially observable world. Most CRL experiments have focused on loss of plasticity -- the inability to keep learning -- in one-off experiments where some unobservable non-stationarity is added to classic fully observable MDPs. Further, these experiments rarely consider the role of partial observability and the importance of CRL agents that use memory or recurrence. One potential reason for this focus on mitigating loss of plasticity without considering partial observability is that many partially-observable CRL environments are prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Forager, a light-weight partially-observable CRL environment with a constant memory footprint. We provide a set of experiments and sample tasks demonstrating that Forager is challenging for current CRL agents and yet also allows for in-depth study of those agents. We demonstrate that agents exhibit loss of plasticity, proposed mitigations can help, but that most useful is to leverage state construction. We conclude with a variant of Forager that generates an unending stream of new tasks to learn that clearly highlights the limitations of current CRL agents.

</details>


### [232] [Metric-Normalized Posterior Leakage (mPL): Attacker-Aligned Privacy for Joint Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01137)
*Gaoyi Chen,Minghao Li,Weishi Shi,Yan Huang,Yusheng Wei,Sourabh Yadav,Chenxi Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出度量归一化后验泄漏（mPL）作为针对联合观测场景的隐私度量，并引入自适应mPL（AmPL）框架，在词嵌入案例中证明其能有效降低联合消费下的隐私泄漏风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量差分隐私（mDP）在联合观测场景下，模型无关的逐条记录保护无法捕捉证据聚合带来的泄漏，导致隐私保证失效。

Method: 提出mPL作为攻击者对后验概率比偏移的距离校准度量；形式化概率有界mPL（PBmPL）；实现AmPL框架，包含扰动、学习型攻击者审计和参数自适应（可选贝叶斯重映射）。

Result: 在词嵌入案例中，神经攻击者在联合消费下违反mPL保护，而AmPL显著降低违规频率，且效用损失较低。

Conclusion: PBmPL是联合消费场景下实用且可认证的隐私保护方案，AmPL能有效平衡隐私与效用。

Abstract: Metric differential privacy (mDP) strengthens local differential privacy (LDP) by scaling noise to semantic distance, but many machine learning (ML) systems are consumed under joint observation, where model-agnostic, per-record guarantees can miss leakage from evidence aggregation. We introduce metric-normalized posterior leakage (mPL), an attacker-aligned, distance-calibrated measure of posterior-odds shift induced by releases, and show that for single or independent releases, uniformly bounding mPL is equivalent to mDP. Under joint observation, however, satisfying mDP may still leave mPL high because learned aggregators compound evidence across correlated items. To make control practical, we formalize probabilistically bounded mPL (PBmPL), which limits how often mPL may exceed a target budget, and we operationalize it via Adaptive mPL (AmPL), a trust-and-verify framework that perturbs, audits with a learned attacker, and adapts parameters (with optional Bayesian remapping) to balance privacy and utility. In a word-embedding case study, neural adversaries violate mPL under joint consumption despite per-record mDP perturbations, whereas AmPL substantially lowers the frequency of such violations with low utility loss, indicating PBmPL as a practical, certifiable protection for joint-consumption settings.

</details>


### [233] [Multi-Perspective Transformers in ARC-AGI-2 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01154)
*Caleb Talley,Vedant Tibrewal,Seun Adekunle,Weiwen Dong,Xinyu Wu,Fariha Sheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用TinyLM模型结合测试时训练（TTT）和专家乘积（POE）方法解决ARC-AGI-2视觉推理谜题，在训练集上达到96.1%准确率，评估集上达到21.7%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC-AGI-2作为衡量机器从有限样例中泛化、理解符号意义并灵活应用规则的人类直觉视觉谜题基准，目前尚无模型能高效解决，亟需新的方法突破。

Method: 采用TinyLM作为基础模型，在测试时进行额外微调，具体包括测试时训练（Test-Time-Training, TTT）和专家乘积（Products of Experts, POE）两种技术。

Result: 训练集准确率96.1%，评估集准确率21.7%。

Conclusion: 测试时微调策略（TTT和POE）对提升模型在ARC-AGI-2上的泛化能力有效，但评估集上的较低准确率表明该基准仍具有很大挑战性。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-2 is a benchmark of human-intuitive visual puzzles that measures a machine's ability to generalize from limited examples, interpret symbolic meaning, and flexibly apply rules in varying contexts. In this paper, we discuss our approach to solving the ARC-AGI-2 puzzles with TinyLM, with additional fine-tuning at test time, including Test-Time-Training (TTT) and Products of Experts (POE). Our model achieves 96.1% accuracy on the training set and 21.7% accuracy on the evaluation set.

</details>


### [234] [Minimizing Collateral Damage in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01167)
*Tam Nguyen,Tu Anh Nguyen,Sina Alemohammad,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化的激活引导方法，通过考虑激活的二阶矩矩阵来最小化非目标特征方向的附带损害，从而实现更精确的LLM行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导方法（如向量加法）会破坏非目标特征方向的排列，造成"附带损害"，因为这类方法隐含假设非目标特征是各向同性的。作者旨在数学形式化该问题并提出更优的解决方案。

Method: 将引导建模为约束优化问题，最小化加权期望平方附带变化，权重使用激活的经验二阶矩矩阵，从而对不同特征方向的扰动施加非均匀惩罚。

Result: 与各向同性方法相比，该方法在实现更精确控制的同时，减少了对无关任务性能的退化。

Conclusion: 通过考虑激活的二阶矩信息，可以显著降低引导干预对非目标特征的负面影响，实现更可控且副作用更小的LLM行为调控。

Abstract: Activation steering is a method for controlling Large Language Model (LLM) behavior by intervening in its internal representations to increase the alignment with a specific target feature direction. However, standard interventions, such as vector addition, often cause ``collateral damage", defined as unintended changes in the alignment of activations along other non-target feature directions. This damage occurs because standard methods implicitly assume the isotropy of non-target features. In this work, we provide a mathematical formalization of collateral damage and introduce a principled framework that models steering as a constrained optimization problem. Our method finds a new activation that minimizes the expected squared collateral change weighted by the empirical second-moment matrix of activations. This weighting encodes the nonuniform cost of the perturbation in different feature directions, in contrast to isotropic approaches that penalize changes uniformly in all feature directions. By accounting for the empirical second-moment of activations, our approach achieves more precise control while reducing the degradation of model performance on unrelated tasks.

</details>


### [235] [A Theory of Generalization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01172)
*Elon Litman,Gabe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度学习泛化的非渐近理论：经验神经正切核将输出空间划分为信号子空间（误差快速消散）和噪声子空间（误差被近零特征值捕获为测试不可见残差）；在信号通道中，小批SGD使群体信号通过快速线性漂移积累，抑制记忆化为慢扩散随机游走。该理论可解释良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差和grokking等现象。此外，推导出无需验证数据的精确种群风险目标函数，在实践中表现为Adam上的SNR预条件子，可将grokking加速5倍、抑制PINNs和隐式神经表示中的记忆化、改进噪声偏好下的DPO微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深度学习提供严格的非渐近泛化理论，解释神经切向核在信号与噪声通道中的不同作用机制，并推导出可直接用于训练的可计算种群风险目标。

Method: 利用经验神经正切核（NTK）的特征分解，将输出空间划分为信号子空间（大特征值方向）和噪声子空间（近零特征值方向）。在信号通道中分析小批SGD的线性漂移与扩散过程；证明即使核在算子范数下O(1)变化（完全特征学习）时泛化仍成立；并基于信号通道噪声推导出精确种群风险目标函数。

Result: 理论统一解释了良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差和grokking等现象；推导的种群风险目标在实践中等价于Adam上的SNR预条件子（仅增加一个状态向量），在实验中实现grokking加速5倍、PINNs和隐式神经表示中记忆化抑制、噪声偏好下DPO微调效果提升且离参考策略近3倍。

Conclusion: 深度学习的泛化可由NTK特征空间中的信号-噪声分离机制精确描述；基于该理论导出的训练时种群风险估计器具有实际应用价值，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: We present a non-asymptotic theory of generalization in deep learning where the empirical neural tangent kernel partitions the output space. In directions corresponding to signal, error dissipates rapidly; in the vast orthogonal dimensions corresponding to noise, the kernel's near-zero eigenvalues trap residual error in a test-invisible reservoir. Within the signal channel, minibatch SGD ensures that coherent population signal accumulates via fast linear drift, while idiosyncratic memorization is suppressed into a slow, diffusive random walk. We prove generalization survives even when the kernel evolves $\mathcal{O}(1)$ in operator norm, the full feature-learning regime. This theory naturally explains disparate phenomena in deep learning theory, such as benign overfitting, double descent, implicit bias, and grokking. Lastly, we derive an exact population-risk objective from a single training run with no validation data, for any architecture, loss, or optimizer, and prove that it measures precisely the noise in the signal channel. This objective reduces in practice to an SNR preconditioner on top of Adam, adding one state vector at no extra cost; it accelerates grokking by $5 \times$, suppresses memorization in PINNs and implicit neural representations, and improves DPO fine-tuning under noisy preferences while staying $3 \times$ closer to the reference policy.

</details>


### [236] [Linear-Readout Floors and Threshold Recovery in Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01192)
*Hector Borobia,Elies Seguí-Mas,Guillermina Tormo-Carbó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了两种看似矛盾的叠加计算容量界限（$O(d^{3/2})$ 与近二次）并非冲突，而是源于不同的接口不变量；通过推导双正交线性读出的Welch型下界，解释了$d^{3/2}$刻度是特定模板的兼容性阈值而非通用上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hänni等人与Adler和Shavit在叠加计算中得出了不同递归容量体制（$O(d^{3/2})$ vs. 近二次），表面上相互矛盾，需要澄清并形式化其区别。

Method: 1) 记录双正交线性读出的秩-迹Welch型下界；2) 分析随机支撑阈值恢复与线性读出在不同稀疏度下的误差行为；3) 将Welch下界与Hänni修正层的容差进行匹配。

Result: 1) 对于$F\gg d$，单位对角线性读出的最坏情况串扰为$\Omega(d^{-1/2})$；2) 在二次特征负载$F=d^2$下，随机支撑阈值恢复在稀疏度$s=O(d/\log d)$时成功；3) 线性读出在Bernoulli稀疏状态下仍产生$\Omega(s/d)$平均每坐标平方误差。

Conclusion: 两个容量结果不矛盾，因为维护了不同的接口不变量；$d^{3/2}$是特定Hänni模板的兼容性阈值，而非通用上限；鲁棒非线性重置超出该模板的范围仍悬而未决。

Abstract: Two recent approaches to computation in superposition reach different recursive capacity regimes: Hänni et al. certify $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$ computable features in width $d$ via an approximate-linear recursive template, while Adler and Shavit reach near-quadratic capacity (up to logarithmic factors) using thresholded Boolean recovery. The main contribution of this paper is conceptual: we argue these results are not contradictory because they maintain different interface invariants, and we formalize the distinction.
  As a tool, we record a rank-trace Welch-type lower bound for biorthogonal linear readouts: for $F \gg d$, the worst-case off-diagonal cross-talk of any unit-diagonal linear readout is $Ω(d^{-1/2})$, and the bound is tight on average for unit-norm tight frames. At quadratic feature load $F=d^2$, random-support threshold recovery succeeds for sparsities $s=O(d/\log d)$, while linear readouts still incur $Ω(s/d)$ average per-coordinate squared error on Bernoulli sparse states. Matching the Welch floor against the published tolerance of the Hänni correction layer explains the $d^{3/2}$ scale as a compatibility threshold for that template, not a universal upper bound. Robust nonlinear reset beyond the Hänni template is left open.

</details>


### [237] [Focus and Dilution: The Multi-stage Learning Process of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01199)
*Zheng-An Chen,Pengxiao Lin,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过梯度流分析，在单层Transformer和马尔可夫数据设定下，识别并严格解释了注意力学习中存在的“聚焦-稀释循环”（focus-dilution cycle），将其分解为嵌入快速低秩化、注意力参数激活并聚焦高频token、注意力扰动引发质量重分配导致聚焦稀释、低频token不对称性打破退化临界点并开启下一循环等阶段，并在合成数据和真实文本数据上验证了该动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前Transformer模型在众多领域取得成功，但其训练动力学尚未被充分理解，缺乏对注意力学习过程的严格理论分析。

Method: 采用梯度流分析，在单层Transformer和马尔可夫数据设定下，通过在临界点附近进行阶段线性化，将聚焦-稀释循环分解为多个可解析的阶段，并结合理论推导与实验验证。

Result: 发现了注意力学习中的聚焦-稀释循环，严格刻画了嵌入低秩化、注意力聚焦高频token、质量重分配稀释注意力、低频token不对称性驱动循环迭代等一系列阶段，并在合成马尔可夫数据及WikiText、TinyStories数据集上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解Transformer注意力机制的训练动力学提供了严格的理论框架，揭示了注意力学习中的周期性演化模式。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, yet our understanding of their training dynamics remains limited. In this work, we identify a recurrent focus-dilution cycle in attention learning and provide a rigorous explanation in a one-layer Transformer setting for Markovian data via gradient-flow analysis. Using stage-wise linearization around critical points, we show that a single focus-dilution cycle can be decomposed into a sequence of distinct stages. First, embedding and projection rapidly condense to a rank-one structure, while attention parameters remain effectively frozen. Then, the attention parameters begin to increase, inducing a frequency-driven focus toward high-frequency tokens. As attention continues to evolve, it generates next-order perturbations in embeddings, leading to a mass-redistribution mechanism that progressively dilutes this focus. Finally, small asymmetries among low-frequency tokens lift a degenerate critical point, opening new embedding directions and initiating the next cycle. Experiments on synthetic Markovian data as well as WikiText and TinyStories corroborate the predicted stages and cyclical dynamics.

</details>


### [238] [Local Hessian Spectral Filtering for Robust Intrinsic Dimension Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01221)
*Genki Osada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部Hessian谱维度（LHSD），通过对log密度Hessian进行谱滤波，切断法向方向的大特征值，从而在高维空间中准确估计局部本征维度，并利用随机Lanczos求积实现线性可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的局部本征维度（LID）估计方法在高维空间中失效，因为大量法向方向的噪声淹没了切向信号。

Method: 对log密度Hessian矩阵应用谱滤波，明确截断与法向方向相关的大特征值，仅计数零曲率切向方向；通过随机Lanczos求积（SLQ）实现，避免显式构建完整Hessian矩阵，达到线性复杂度O(D)。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验证实了LHSD的优越鲁棒性，并在大规模扩散模型中成功检测记忆化现象。

Conclusion: LHSD通过谱滤波策略有效解决了高维LID估计的信噪比问题，且计算高效，具有实用价值。

Abstract: While diffusion models enable new approaches for estimating Local Intrinsic Dimension (LID), existing methods fail in high-dimensional spaces where noise from vast normal directions overwhelms the tangent signal. We propose Local Hessian Spectral Dimension (LHSD), which resolves this by applying spectral filtering to the log-density Hessian, explicitly cutting off large eigenvalues associated with normal directions to count zero-curvature tangent directions. Implemented using Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature (SLQ), LHSD avoids full Hessian construction, achieving linear scalability with dimension $D$. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm LHSD's superior robustness and its utility in detecting memorization in large-scale diffusion models.

</details>


### [239] [Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows for Spatiotemporal Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01226)
*Keyan Chen,Qiwei Yuan,Zhitong Xu,Bin Shen,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCH框架，一种基于层次流匹配的时空事件建模方法，通过混合掩码策略实现任意条件推断，在预测、逆推断和轨迹重建任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点过程模型依赖强结构假设，局限于自回归的事件逐次预测，难以支持逆推断、轨迹重建和缺失位置恢复等更广泛的推理任务。

Method: 提出ARCH（Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows）框架，采用历史编码器-生成解码器架构，结合层次流匹配与混合掩码策略，实现对任意观测事件的灵活条件化，统一支持预测、逆推断和部分轨迹恢复。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，ARCH在预测和条件推断任务中均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ARCH能够有效捕获复杂事件分布，同时支持可处理的、准确的条件强度计算，为时空事件建模提供统一的灵活框架。

Abstract: Events in spatiotemporal systems are ubiquitous, yet modeling their complex distributions remains challenging. Existing point process models often rely on strong structural assumptions and are typically limited to autoregressive, event-by-event prediction. As a result, they struggle to support broader inference tasks such as inverse inference, trajectory reconstruction, and recovery of missing event locations. We introduce Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows (ARCH), a hierarchical flow matching framework for spatiotemporal event modeling. ARCH is expressive enough to capture complex event distributions while enabling tractable and accurate computation of conditional intensities, which quantify instantaneous event risk. Built on a history-encoder-generative-decoder architecture, ARCH introduces a hybrid masking strategy for flexible conditioning on arbitrary observed events. This enables a unified treatment of forecasting, inverse inference, and partial trajectory recovery within a single framework. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that ARCH consistently outperforms existing baselines across both prediction and conditional inference tasks.

</details>


### [240] [Attention Sinks in Massively Multilingual Neural Machine Translation:Discovery, Analysis, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01229)
*Hillary Mutisya,John Mugane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NLLB-200 (600M)模型中，非内容标记（句尾符、语言标签、标点）占据了83%-91%的跨注意力权重，这种“注意力沉没”现象导致原始指标严重低估内容层面相似性（36.7% vs. 70.7%），通过内容过滤可恢复被掩盖的语言结构信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMT可解释性研究依赖交叉注意力分析，但未发现词汇设计导致的系统性伪影（非内容标记占据绝大部分注意力），使得未经校正的分析结果不可靠。

Method: 1) 统计NLLB-200中各标记类型的注意力占比，定位注意力沉没现象；2) 提出内容过滤方法（移除非内容标记并重归一化）；3) 在非洲语言（斯瓦希里语、基库尤语、索马里语、卢奥语）和非非洲语言（德语、土耳其语、中文、印地语）共1000个平行句子上验证。

Result: 1) 非内容标记占据83%-91%交叉注意力；2) 原始内容相似度36.7%，过滤后升至70.7%；3) 过滤后恢复16.9个百分点的教师强制与生成模式差距；4) 清晰的语言家族聚类；5) 发现索马里语SOV语序与单调对齐的矛盾关联。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力分析必须进行内容过滤才能可靠用于NMT可解释性研究，研究团队已开源过滤工具和校正数据集以支持可重复研究。

Abstract: Cross-attention patterns in neural machine translation (NMT) are widely used to study how multilingual models align linguistic structure. We report a systematic artifact in cross-attention analysis of NLLB-200 (600M): non-content tokens - primarily end-of-sequence tokens, language tags, and punctuation - capture 83 percent to 91 percent of total cross-attention mass. We term these "attention sinks," extending findings from LLMs [Xiao et al., 2023] to NMT cross-attention and identifying a causal mechanism rooted in vocabulary design rather than position bias. This artifact causes raw metrics to underestimate content-level similarity by nearly half (36.7 percent raw vs. 70.7 percent filtered), rendering uncorrected analyses unreliable. To address this, we validate a content-only filtering methodology that removes non-content tokens and renormalizes the distribution. Applying this to 1,000 parallel sentences across African languages (Swahili, Kikuyu, Somali, Luo) and non-African benchmarks (German, Turkish, Chinese, Hindi), we confirm the artifact is universal and recover masked linguistic signals: a 16.9 percentage-point gap between teacher-forcing and generation modes, clear language-family clustering in attention entropy, and a hidden Somali paradox linking SOV word order to monotonic alignment. We release our filtering toolkit and corrected datasets to support reproducible interpretability research on multilingual NMT.

</details>


### [241] [CombinationTS: A Modular Framework for Understanding Time-Series Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01231)
*Xiaorui Wang,Fanda Fan,Chenxi Wang,Yuxuan Yang,Rui Tang,Kuoyu Gao,Simiao Pang,Yuanfeng Shang,Zhipeng Liu,Wanling Gao,Lei Wang,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CombinationTS，一个用于时间序列预测模型的可解释性评估框架，通过将模型分解为正交模块并量化其边际性能和稳定性，揭示了"恒等悖论"：当数据视图（Embedding）设计良好时，无参数的恒等编码器可以匹配甚至超越复杂编码器，并且输入变换引入的结构先验比增加编码器复杂度能实现更好的性能-稳定性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测领域的进展虽然模型架构日益复杂，但许多SOTA改进在统计上并不稳健，且性能增益往往被错误归因。作者认为需要从模型选择转向模块归因，识别真正驱动性能的组件。

Method: 提出CombinationTS框架：将预测模型分解为五个正交模块（输入变换、嵌入、编码器、解码器、输出变换），在共享评估条件空间下评估各组件，通过边际化性能（μ）和稳定性（σ）进行量化归因。

Result: 发现"恒等悖论"：只要Embedding设计得当，无参数的恒等编码器（Identity Encoder）可与复杂编码器性能相当甚至更优；输入变换引入的结构先验比增加编码器复杂度能带来更优的性能-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 有必要建立基于模块归因的架构必要性基线，避免盲目追求复杂模型结构。

Abstract: Recent progress in time-series forecasting has led to rapidly increasing architectural complexity, yet many reported State-of-the-Art gains are statistically fragile or misattributed. We argue that progress requires a shift from model selection to modular attribution, identifying which components truly drive performance. We propose CombinationTS, a self-contained probabilistic evaluation framework that decomposes forecasting models into orthogonal modules--Input Transformation, Embedding, Encoder, Decoder, and Output Transformation--and evaluates them under a shared evaluation condition space. By quantifying each component via marginalized performance ($μ$) and stability ($σ$), CombinationTS enables robust attribution beyond fragile point estimates. Through large-scale paired evaluation, we uncover the Identity Paradox: once the data view (Embedding) is well-designed, a parameter-free Identity Encoder often matches or outperforms complex backbones. We further show that explicit structural priors introduced via Input Transformations yield a more favorable performance-stability trade-off than increasing Encoder complexity, establishing a principled baseline for architectural necessity.

</details>


### [242] [Rhamba: Region-Aware Hybrid Attention-Mamba Framework for Self-Supervised Learning in Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01240)
*Ruthwik Reddy Doodipala,Pankaj Pandey,Pratheek Eranki,Carolina Torres-Rojas,Manob Jyoti Saikia,Ranganatha Sitaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rhamba框架，结合区域感知掩码和混合Attention-Mamba架构用于fMRI自监督预训练，在ABIDE数据集上预训练，在COBRE和ADHD-200数据集上微调，混合架构MA配置取得最佳平均AUROC，并超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练在大规模神经影像分析中前景广阔，但区域感知掩码和混合序列模型的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Rhamba框架，采用解剖引导的掩码策略（Any、Majority、Pure三种空间特异性递增的掩码方式）与混合Attention-Mamba架构。预训练于ABIDE数据集，使用区域对齐的patch嵌入。设计了四种架构变体：纯Mamba、交错Mamba与Attention的Alternate架构、两种混合编码器-解码器（AM和MA）。微调后使用Integrated Gradients进行可解释性分析。

Result: 掩码策略强烈影响重构行为（损失排序：Any > Majority > Pure），但该趋势未直接转化为下游性能。MA混合架构在双数据集上取得最高平均AUROC，Rhamba整体优于现有方法。最佳性能取决于掩码策略与架构的交互，而非单一配置。

Conclusion: Rhamba提供了一个灵活框架，在大规模fMRI表示学习中平衡了可解释性、可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is promising for large-scale neuroimaging, yet the impact of region-aware masking and hybrid sequence modeling remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce Rhamba, a region-aware pretraining framework that integrates anatomically guided masking with hybrid Attention-Mamba architectures for resting state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) analysis. Models were pretrained on the ABIDE dataset using region-aligned patch embeddings and three masking strategies (Any, Majority, and Pure) with increasing spatial specificity. We evaluated four architectural variants: a Mamba only model, an Alternate architecture with interleaved Mamba and Attention blocks, and two hybrid encoder-decoder configurations (Attention-Mamba (AM) and Mamba-Attention (MA)). The pretrained models were fine-tuned on downstream classification tasks using the COBRE and ADHD-200 datasets for schizophrenia and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder discrimination. We employed Integrated Gradients, an explainable AI method, to identify the brain regions contributing to model predictions. Masking strategy strongly influenced reconstruction behavior, with reconstruction loss following a consistent ordering (Any > Majority > Pure). However, this trend did not directly translate into downstream performance, where differences were modest and dataset-dependent. The hybrid architecture with the MA configuration achieved the highest average AUROC across both datasets, and Rhamba outperformed state-of-the-art methods in comparative evaluation. Region-wise analysis showed that peak performance depends on the interaction between masking strategy and architecture rather than a single dominant configuration. Overall, Rhamba offers a flexible framework for balancing interpretability, scalability, and performance in large-scale fMRI representation learning.

</details>


### [243] [S^3-R1: Learning to Retrieve and Answer Step-by-Step with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01248)
*Harsh Goel,Akhil Udathu,Susmija Jabireddy,Pradnesh Kalkar,Atharva Parulekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S^3-R1是一个结合合成数据生成与稠密奖励信号的框架，通过程序化生成多跳问题及检索验证步骤筛选中等难度问题，并设计同时评估中间搜索质量和最终答案正确性的奖励结构，有效缓解稀疏奖励带来的信用分配问题，在跨领域数据集上实现了高达10%的鲁棒泛化改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练模型因稀疏的结果导向奖励和缺乏涵盖不同难度问题的训练数据而表现不佳，无法利用工具进行深层搜索以收集问答证据。

Method: 提出S^3-R1框架：1) 基于已有文档程序化生成并筛选多样化的多跳问题，通过检索验证步骤孤立出中等难度问题；2) 构建同时评估中间搜索质量和最终答案正确性的奖励结构，缓解稀疏奖励导致的信用分配问题。

Result: S^3-R1在跨领域数据集上实现了高达10%的鲁棒泛化改进，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过数据驱动的方法结合更稠密的学习信号（合成数据生成+中间搜索质量奖励），能有效提升模型在工具辅助搜索问答场景下的表现和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has enabled newer capabilities in models, such as agentic tool-use for search. However, these models struggle primarily due to limitations with sparse outcome-based rewards and a lack of training data that encapsulates questions of differing hardness, which results in models not performing deeper searches with tools to collect evidence for question-answering. To address these limitations, we introduce S^3-R1 (Synthetic data and stabilized Search R1), a framework that couples a data-centric approach with denser learning signals. We first develop a synthetic generation and curation pipeline that programmatically derives diverse, multi-hop questions from existing documents. This pipeline incorporates a retrieval-based verification step to specifically isolate questions of intermediate difficulty. We then pair this expanded training set with a reward structure that evaluates both intermediate search quality and the correctness of the final answer. This setup directly mitigates the credit assignment problems inherent to sparse rewards. Our evaluations show that S^3-R1 outperforms existing baselines by learning more effective search and synthesis strategies, yielding up to a 10% improvement in robust generalization on out-of-domain datasets.

</details>


### [244] [Activation Compression in LLMs: Theoretical Analysis and Efficient Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01255)
*Wen-Da Wei,Han-Bin Fang,Yang-Di Liu,Jiang-Xin Shi,James Kwok,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出理论框架证明无偏激活压缩对线性算子安全，并基于此设计激活-梯度联合压缩方法，在不增加额外计算和梯度误差的前提下压缩线性层梯度，在Qwen和LLaMA模型上验证了方法的准确性和压缩效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练内存占用极高，现有内存高效方法主要关注梯度和优化器状态，而激活压缩由于缺乏针对LLM的理论保障尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 首先建立理论框架，证明无偏激活压缩对线性算子安全但对非线性算子有问题；推导梯度方差界和收敛保证；提出激活-梯度联合压缩方法，复用低秩激活因子压缩线性层梯度。

Result: 在Qwen和LLaMA模型上的预训练和微调基准实验中，验证了理论正确性，方法在准确性和压缩效率上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 无偏激活压缩不改变线性算子的收敛率，所提方法能有效降低训练内存占用且保持模型性能。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) is highly memory-intensive, as training must store not only weights and optimizer states but also intermediate activations for backpropagation. While existing memory-efficient methods largely focus on gradients and optimizer states, activation compression is less well established due to the lack of LLM-tailored theory and guarantees. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework showing that activation compression is safe for linear operators when activation compression is unbiased, but problematic for nonlinear ones. We further derive gradient variance bound and establish convergence guarantees for applying activation compression to all linear operators under the standard $L$-smoothness assumption, showing that it does not change the convergence rate. Guided by the theory, we propose an activation-gradient co-compression method that reuses low-rank activation factors to compress linear-layer gradients without extra computation or additional gradient error. We conduct extensive experiments on Qwen and LLaMA models using a pretraining benchmark and multiple fine-tuning benchmarks to validate our theory and demonstrate competitive performance of our method in both accuracy and compression efficiency. We provide our code in the supplementary material for reproducibility.

</details>


### [245] [Continuous Temporal Representations of Event-Based Signals via Interference-Based Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01270)
*Magnus Bengtsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干涉波动表示的连续时间建模框架，将事件驱动的生物电信号（如sEMG）映射到复值潜在波动场中，通过相位调制编码时间结构，无需显式循环或因果状态传播，在生物力学控制任务中实现了优于纯实值表示的特征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离散或纯实值表示难以有效建模表面肌电信号（sEMG）等事件驱动生物过程中呈现的非同步、高度结构化的激活模式。

Method: 构建基于干涉的波动表示框架：将事件输入映射到复值潜在波动场，通过相位调制和潜在分量之间的相互作用编码时间结构；将波动场投影到能量域，诱导出能在有限观测窗口内同时捕获时间定位和关系依赖的结构化激活模式。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提干涉波动模型在表示质量上优于纯实值表示，同时保持了适合实际部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该连续表示方法特别适合事件驱动的生物信号处理，能支持高效梯度优化和鲁棒特征提取，适用于假肢和外骨骼等生物力学系统的下游控制任务。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal signals arising from event-driven biological processes, such as surface electromyography (sEMG), exhibit asynchronous and highly structured activation patterns that are challenging to model using conventional discrete or purely real-valued representations.
  In this work, we propose a continuous temporal modeling framework based on interference-based wave representations. The approach maps event-like input signals into a complex-valued latent wave field, where temporal structure is encoded through phase modulation and interactions between latent components.
  By projecting the resulting wave field onto an energy domain, the model induces structured activation patterns that capture both temporal localization and relational dependencies within finite observation windows, without relying on explicit recurrence or causal state propagation.
  The proposed formulation is particularly suited for event-driven biosignals, where continuous representations enable efficient gradient-based optimization and robust feature extraction. In particular, the method is designed to support learning from sEMG data for downstream control tasks in biomechanical systems, such as prosthetic devices and exoskeletons.
  Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed interference-based wave model provides improved representation quality compared to purely real-valued representations, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [246] [Autonomous Drift Learning in Data Streams: A Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01295)
*Xiaoyu Yang,En Yu,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三维分类法来系统化非平稳环境下的学习系统研究，将漂移分为时间流漂移、数据流漂移和模型流漂移三个维度，并基于此框架回顾了193项研究，指出了关键挑战，为构建自进化学习系统提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究主要关注概念漂移（时间维度上的数据分布变化），但随着学习系统日益自主和复杂，仅适应时间非平稳性已不足够，需要更全面的分类框架来统合漂移适应、持续学习和时间泛化等碎片化研究范式。

Method: 提出三维分类法：(1) 时间流漂移——区分随机任意模式与结构节律动态；(2) 数据流漂移——区分特征表示漂移与底层语义漂移；(3) 模型流漂移——通过顺序可塑性、分散异质性和策略不稳定性刻画模型内部内生发散。基于此框架系统回顾193项代表性研究。

Result: 系统化梳理了非平稳学习领域的研究现状，识别出关键开放挑战，并证明了所提分类法能够有效桥接此前碎片化的研究范式。

Conclusion: 该三维分类法为构建能够在持续变化中自主学习的自进化智能系统提供了系统性的路线图。

Abstract: In the pursuit of autonomous learning systems, the foundational assumption of stationarity, the premise that data distributions and model behaviors remain constant, is fundamentally untenable. Historically, the research community has addressed non-stationary environments almost exclusively under the scope of concept drift, focusing primarily on temporal shifts in streams. However, as learning systems become increasingly autonomous and complex, merely adapting to temporal non-stationarity is no longer sufficient. Evolving beyond this traditional perspective, we propose a novel, three-dimensional taxonomy that systematizes the field based on the operational state of the system. First, time stream drift distinguishes between stochastic arbitrary patterns and structural rhythmic dynamics. Second, data stream drift disentangles shifts in feature representations, identified as representation drift, from changes in underlying semantics, recognized as semantic drift. Third, model stream drift characterizes the internal endogenous divergence of learning systems through the lenses of sequential plasticity, decentralized heterogeneity, and policy instability. Based on this framework, we systematically review 193 representative studies and identify key open challenges. By bridging the fragmented paradigms of drift adaptation, continual learning, and temporal generalization, this survey outlines a roadmap for building self-evolving intelligent systems capable of learning autonomously through continuous change.

</details>


### [247] [GA-VisAgent: A Multi-Agent application for code generation and visualization in interactive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01299)
*Wang Jian,Zhou Jianbo,Xiong Yuhao,Liu Zhenxia,Luo Wen,Yuan LinWang,Yu ZhaoYuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GA-VisAgent，一个基于几何代数大语言模型的多智能体交互学习应用，通过任务规划与ReAct推理策略，将复杂几何代数操作分解为五个标准化子任务，实现自然语言到可执行代码的自动生成与交互式可视化，在40个典型共形几何代数任务中达到90%代码生成成功率，比GPT-4o提升70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何代数软件包依赖手动编写脚本，学习曲线高；基于LLM的方法生成GA脚本时逻辑错误频发、准确率低。亟需一种降低学习门槛、提升代码生成准确率的自动化方法。

Method: 构建GAGPT基础模型，设计多智能体系统GA-VisAgent，集成任务规划机制与ReAct推理策略，将复杂GA操作分解为几何积、旋转、反射等五个标准化子任务，支持自然语言和数学公式输入，自动生成可执行代码并附带交互式可视化。

Result: 在40个共形几何代数典型任务上，GA-VisAgent代码生成成功率达90%，相较于GPT-4o提升70%。

Conclusion: 该方法为几何代数教学及相关数学概念的可视化工具开发提供了一种可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Geometric Algebra (GA) presents challenges to learners due to its highly abstract mathematical structure and complex operational rules, as translating algebraic manipulations into concrete geometric interpretations is a non-intuitive process when developing related code. Currently, some existing GA software packages rely on manually written scripts for code generation and visualization, but their high learning curve hinders widespread adoption. Meanwhile, methods based on Large Language Models (LLMs) often produce logical errors when generating specific GA scripts, such as GAALOPScript, resulting in generally low accuracy. To address these issues, this study proposes GA-VisAgent -- a multi-agent interactive learning application for GA code generation and visualization -- building upon a Geometric algebra large language model (GAGPT). Integrating task planning mechanisms with ReAct reasoning strategies, GA-VisAgent can decompose complex operations into five standardized subtasks, including core operations like geometric products, rotations, and reflections. It supports natural language and mathematical formulas as input to automatically generate executable code, accompanied by interactive visualizations to aid user comprehension. Experimental results show that GA-VisAgent achieved a 90% code generation success rate across 40 typical Conformal GA tasks, representing a 70% improvement over GPT-4o. This application introduces an extensible new paradigm for teaching GA and developing visualization tools for related mathematical concepts. The online service for this project will be available at http://gagis.cn/gacrac.

</details>


### [248] [GraphSculptor: Sculpting Pre-training Coreset for Graph Self-supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01310)
*Chuang Liu,Zelin Yao,Xueqi Ma,Luzhi Wang,Mukun Chen,Pinghua Xu,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphSculptor提出了一种无需标签的图预训练核心集构建方法，通过内在结构特征和上下文语义两个互补视角选择代表性样本，仅用10%的数据即可达到全量数据99.6%的性能，同时减少近90%的预训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图自监督学习依赖大规模无标注数据集，计算成本高昂，而经验证据表明这些数据存在大量冗余——均匀采样50%的图仍能保留96%以上的下游性能。现有方法要么依赖额外的训练时信号，要么仅局限于拓扑统计信息，缺乏一个无需标签的通用解决方案。

Method: GraphSculptor从两个互补视角构建核心集：1）内在结构多样性：使用图的统计量构造结构特征向量；2）上下文语义多样性：利用预训练语言模型对图到文本生成的描述进行编码。将两类信号融合到统一的度量空间中，执行聚类感知的选择以保留联合结构-语义多样性，并推导了核心集与全量数据预训练之间损失差距的理论界。

Result: 实验表明：10%的核心集即可达到全量数据99.6%的性能，预训练时间减少近90%。

Conclusion: GraphSculptor提供了一种可扩展的数据高效图预训练解决方案，通过挖掘数据冗余有效降低计算成本而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning typically relies on large-scale unlabeled datasets, heavily inflating computational costs. However, empirical evidence suggests that these datasets contain substantial redundancy-our analysis reveals that uniformly subsampling 50% of graphs retains over 96% of downstream performance. To exploit this redundancy, we introduce GraphSculptor for pre-training coreset construction. Unlike methods dependent on additional training-time signals or limited solely to topological statistics, GraphSculptor provides a label-free solution that constructs coresets via two complementary perspectives: intrinsic structure and contextual semantics. Concretely, structural diversity is quantified using intrinsic graph statistics, yielding a structural feature vector for each graph, while semantic diversity is captured by utilizing a pre-trained language model to encode descriptions generated via graph-to-text. GraphSculptor integrates these signals into a unified metric space and performs cluster-aware selection to preserve joint structural-semantic diversity. We further derive a theoretical bound on the loss gap between coreset and full-data pre-training, offering theoretical motivation for our selection formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GraphSculptor effectively sculpts the dataset: a 10% coreset achieves 99.6% of full-data performance while reducing pre-training time by nearly 90%, offering a scalable solution for data-efficient graph pre-training.

</details>


### [249] [The Partial Testimony of Logs: Evaluation of Language Model Generation under Confounded Model Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01311)
*Jikai Jin,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三源设计方案（OBS+EXP+SIM），通过小规模随机实验和离线模拟器消除观测日志中的混杂偏差，从而实现对语言模型的无偏离线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估语言模型时，由于模型选择与用户判断存在混杂因素（confounding），导致观测日志中的分数比较存在偏差。小规模随机实验可以打破这种偏差，但实验稀缺且昂贵，因此需要一种结合观测数据与有限实验数据的方法。

Method: 提出三源设计：大规模混杂观测日志（OBS）提供规模，小规模随机实验（EXP）提供无偏评分，离线模拟器（SIM）在缓存上下文上重放候选模型。核心是识别定理：随机实验和模拟器足以恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于降低估计误差。评估了六种估计器家族。

Result: 在半合成验证和两个真实任务缓存的基准（摘要生成和代码生成）上评估了六种估计器家族。没有一种估计器在所有设定下占优；相对性能取决于无偏EXP监督的量和目标奖励与OBS衍生结构的一致性程度。

Conclusion: 随机实验和离线模拟器相结合可以恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于降方差而非纠偏。不同估计器在不同设定下各有优劣，选择合适的估计器需视具体数据条件而定。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of language models from usage logs is biased when model choice is confounded: the same user-side factors that influence which model is used can also influence how its output is judged, so raw comparisons of logged scores mix self-selected populations rather than estimating a common quantity of interest. A small randomized experiment can break this bias by overriding model choice, but in practice such experiments are scarce and costly. We study a three-source design that combines a large confounded observational log (OBS) for scale, a small randomized experiment (EXP) for unconfounded scoring, and an offline simulator (SIM) that replays candidate models on cached contexts. Our main result is an identification theorem showing that the randomized experiment and the simulator are together enough to recover causal model values; the observational log enters only afterward, to reduce estimation error rather than to make the causal comparison valid. Six estimator families are evaluated in a controlled semi-synthetic validation and in two real-task cached benchmarks for summarization and coding. No family dominates every regime; relative performance depends on the amount of unbiased EXP supervision and on how closely the target reward aligns with OBS-derived structure.

</details>


### [250] [Robust Parameter Learning for Uncertain MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01339)
*Yannik Schnitzer,Alessandro Abate,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出使用参数化MDP学习未知MDP，通过将统计不确定性投影到参数空间来保留转移依赖关系，并构建多面体外近似实现高效求解，相比传统区间方法产生更紧的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于不确定MDP的学习方法独立量化各转移概率的不确定性，忽略了因共享潜在量而导致的代数依赖关系，导致不确定性估计过于保守。

Method: 1) 使用参数化MDP建模，转移概率表示为参数集上的表达式；2) 将经验转移频率的统计不确定性投影到pMDP的参数空间，构建PAC不确定性模型；3) 提出层次化声学多面体外近似方法处理求解挑战。

Result: 所提方法相比经典基于区间的不确定MDP学习方法产生了显著更紧的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 通过保留转移间的代数依赖关系，参数化MDP方法能够在保持PAC保证的同时获得更精确的不确定性刻画，是对传统独立区间方法的有效改进。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to verifying unknown Markov decision processes (MDPs) often employ uncertain MDPs. These models use, for example, confidence intervals to capture transition uncertainty and allow synthesis of policies that are robust to this uncertainty. However, this approach typically quantifies uncertainty independently for individual transition probabilities, ignoring dependencies due to shared latent quantities. We propose to learn such models using parametric MDPs (pMDPs), where transition probabilities are expressions over a set of parameters. We project statistical uncertainty from empirical transition frequencies onto the pMDP's parameter space, yielding a probably approximately correct (PAC) uncertainty model for the underlying MDP that respects the algebraic dependencies between transitions. The resulting models are algorithmically challenging to solve, so we propose a hierarchy of sound polytopic outer approximations of the induced confidence set. We implement and evaluate our approach, demonstrating substantially tighter uncertainty estimates than classical interval-based uncertain MDP learning techniques.

</details>


### [251] [Model-Based Proactive Cost Generation for Learning Safe Policies Offline with Limited Violation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01356)
*Ruiqi Xue,Lei Yuan,Kainuo Cheng,Jing-Wen Yang,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PROCO提出了一种基于模型的离线安全强化学习框架，通过利用大语言模型中的自然语言知识构建保守成本函数，在缺乏违规样本的离线数据中实现约束满足策略学习，显著降低了部署时的约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险安全关键决策场景中，在线试错不可行，导致离线数据集很少或没有不安全样本。现有方法常将所有数据视为均匀安全，忽略了安全但不可行的状态（当前满足约束但几步内必然违反的状态），导致部署失败。

Method: PROCO首先从离线数据学习动力学模型，然后利用LLM中不安全状态的自然语言知识构建保守成本函数（无需观测违规样本即可进行风险评估），再通过基于模型的轨迹生成合成多样化的反事实不安全样本，支持可靠的可行性识别和可行性引导的策略学习。

Result: 在Safety-Gymnasium一系列仅包含安全或最小风险训练数据的任务中，PROCO与多种离线安全RL算法无缝集成，相比原始方法和其他行为克隆基线，持续展现出更少的约束违反和更高的安全性能。

Conclusion: PROCO有效解决了数据稀缺场景下的离线安全强化学习问题，利用LLM知识弥补数据不足，实现了无需风险交互的约束满足策略学习。

Abstract: Learning constraint-satisfying policies from offline data without risky online interaction is crucial for safety-critical decision making. Conventional methods typically learn cost value functions from abundant unsafe samples to define safety boundaries and penalize violations. However, in high-stakes scenarios, risky trial-and-error is infeasible, yielding datasets with few or no unsafe samples. Under this limitation, existing approaches often treat all data as uniformly safe, overlooking safe-but-infeasible states - states that currently satisfy constraints but inevitably violate them within a few steps - leading to deployment failures. Drawing inspiration from the concept of knowledge-data integration, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to incorporate natural language knowledge into the policy to address this challenge. Specifically, we propose PROCO, a model-based offline safe reinforcement learning (RL) framework tailored to datasets largely free of violations. PROCO first learns a dynamics model from offline data and constructs a conservative cost function by grounding natural-language knowledge of unsafe states in LLMs, enabling risk estimation even without observed violations. Using the cost function and learned model, PROCO performs model-based rollouts to synthesize diverse counterfactual unsafe samples, supporting reliable feasibility identification and feasibility-guided policy learning. Across a range of Safety-Gymnasium tasks with exclusively safe or minimally risky training data, PROCO integrates seamlessly with a variety of offline safe RL algorithms and consistently demonstrates reduced constraint violations and improved safety performance compared to both the original methods and other behavior cloning baselines.

</details>


### [252] [Decision-Focused Learning via Tangent-Space Projection of Prediction Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01361)
*Junhyeong Lee,Sangjin Jin,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEAR方法，通过将预测误差投影到活动约束的切空间来近似遗憾梯度，避免了通过求解器求导或替代损失，在LP/QP任务中实现了最佳决策质量和最高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习的遗憾梯度计算通常需要微分求解器（计算昂贵）或依赖替代损失（偏离真实目标），缺乏简洁高效的替代方案。

Method: 在活动约束局部稳定性假设下，推导出遗憾梯度的几何特征——等于MSE梯度投影到活动约束切空间，提出PEAR方法，通过约化活动约束线性系统计算遗憾梯度。

Result: 在LP基准和真实QP任务中，PEAR在所有基线中取得了最佳决策质量，同时计算效率最高，且在约束偏移下仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 遗憾梯度可通过过滤MSE梯度中决策无关成分获得，PEAR提供了一种更简单、更直接的替代方案，无需求解器迭代或额外优化求解。

Abstract: Decision-Focused Learning (DFL) trains predictors to improve downstream decision quality, but computing regret gradients typically requires differentiating through solvers or relying on surrogate losses, which can be computationally expensive or deviate from the true objective. We show that, under standard regularity with locally stable active constraints, the regret gradient admits a closed-form geometric characterization, equivalent to the prediction error projected onto the tangent space of active constraints, scaled by local curvature. This reveals that regret gradients can be obtained by filtering decision-irrelevant components from the MSE gradient, providing a simpler and more direct alternative to existing approaches. Based on this, we propose PEAR (Projected Error As Regret-gradient), which computes regret gradients via a reduced linear system over active constraints, avoiding differentiation through solver iterations or additional optimization solves. Experiments on LP benchmarks and a real-world QP task show that PEAR achieves the best decision quality among all baselines while being the most computationally efficient, with gains that persist under constraint shifts.

</details>


### [253] [Toward a foundational thermal model for residential buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01364)
*Ting-Yu Dai,Kingsley Nweye,Dev Niyogi,Zoltan Nagy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息增强的Transformer架构，用于构建通用建筑热模型。该模型在CityLearn数据集上跨越247栋住宅建筑和三个气候区进行测试，在一步预测中取得了0.30°C（德克萨斯）和0.29°C（佛蒙特）的RMSE，超越了传统基线和时序基础模型，并展示了零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能源领域缺乏一个基础热模型——即一个能够泛化到不同建筑、气候和控制策略而无需针对建筑进行校准的预训练模型。现有方法依赖于建筑特定的校准，缺乏通用性。

Method: 采用物理信息增强的Transformer架构，将领域知识（如导数增强和基于欧拉方法的数值积分）嵌入到仅解码器框架中；从仿真模型中提取静态建筑特征；使用旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）注意力机制捕捉时间依赖性。

Result: 在CityLearn数据集的247栋住宅建筑（三个气候区）上，一步预测RMSE在德克萨斯为0.30°C，佛蒙特为0.29°C；优于传统基线和微调后的时序基础模型；在仅用两栋建筑训练后即可零样本泛化到未见过的建筑和气候区。

Conclusion: 物理信息增强的架构原则是构建通用建筑热模型的有前景的基础，尽管当前工作局限于仿真住宅建筑数据。

Abstract: The building energy community lacks a foundational thermal model, i.e., a single pretrained model capable of generalizing across diverse buildings, climates, and control strategies without building-specific calibration. Achieving this vision requires architectural principles that capture universal thermal dynamics rather than memorizing building-specific patterns. We take a step toward this goal by presenting a physics-informed transformer architecture that embeds domain knowledge, e.g., derivative enrichment and Euler-based numerical integration, into a decoder-only framework. We incorporate static building features extracted from simulation models and employ Rotary Position Embedding attention to capture temporal dependencies. Evaluated on the CityLearn dataset spanning 247 residential buildings across three climate zones, our model achieves one-step prediction accuracy (RMSE of 0.30°C in Texas, 0.29°C in Vermont) while outperforming both traditional baselines and fine-tuned Time-Series Foundation Models. We also demonstrate zero-shot transferability: models trained on as few as two buildings generalize to unseen buildings and climate zones without fine-tuning. Despite the limitation of simulated residential buildings, our results establish physics-informed architectural principles as a promising foundation for universal building thermal models.

</details>


### [254] [Quantifying Multimodal Capabilities: Formal Generalization Guarantees in Pairwise Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01424)
*Richeng Zhou,Xuelin Zhang,Liyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多模态度量学习模型进行了细粒度的理论分析，建立了不同模态子集对应的函数类之间的层次关系，通过配对复杂度分析推导出新的泛化误差界，揭示了模态数量与粒度对模型性能的联合影响，证明了细粒度模态特征通过增强模态互补性降低了假设空间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习在实际应用中常遇到模态数据不完整或冗余的问题，而现有研究对模态选择与算法性能之间的关系缺乏理论理解，存在关键的理论空白。

Method: 建立了不同模态子集对应函数类之间的层次关系；量化了学习映射与真实映射之间的差异；在多模态学习框架内进行了配对复杂度（pairwise complexity）的严格分析，推导了泛化误差的上界和下界。

Result: 推导出了新的泛化误差界，揭示了模态数量与粒度对模型性能的联合影响；证明细粒度模态特征通过增强模态互补性降低了假设空间的复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作为改进多模态学习系统的收敛速度和准确率提供了理论基础和实践启示。

Abstract: Multimodal learning leverages the integration of diverse data modalities to enhance performance in complex tasks. Yet, it frequently encounters incomplete or redundant modality data in real-world scenarios. This paper presents a fine-grained theoretical analysis of the generalization properties of multimodal metric learning models, addressing critical gaps in understanding the relationship between modality selection and algorithmic performance. We establish hierarchical relationships between function classes corresponding to different modality subsets and quantify the discrepancy between learned mappings and ground truth. Through rigorous analysis of pairwise complexity within the multimodal learning framework, we derive novel generalization error bounds that reveal the joint impact of modality quantity and granularity on model performance. Our theoretical findings on both upper and lower bounds demonstrate that incorporating fine-grained modality features reduces the complexity of the hypothesis space by enhancing modality complementarity. This work offers both theoretical foundations and practical implications for improving convergence rates and accuracy in multimodal learning systems.

</details>


### [255] [Barriers to Counterfactual Credit Attribution for Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01425)
*Aloni Cohen,Chenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了反事实信用归因（CCA）在生成模型中的应用，发现在自回归模型中，CCA不具有像差分隐私那样的组合性质，且对已有模型进行CCA改造需要指数级查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在引用前人工作方面存在缺陷，作者希望建立一种技术条件（CCA）来形式化地确保生成模型对对其输出有显著影响的先前工作给予信用归因。

Method: 论文从两个角度分析自回归模型的CCA实现：1) 证明CCA在下一个词预测器上不满足自回归组合性质；2) 提出"retrofitting"方法（对已有模型添加信用归因），并证明其在弱最优性条件下需要指数级查询复杂度。

Result: 1) CCA不像差分隐私那样可以组合，即使每个step的预测器满足CCA，整个自回归模型也不满足CCA；2) 对黑盒模型进行CCA改造需要指数级（相对于输出长度）的查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 构建CCA生成模型存在根本性困难，简单的逐token组合或事后改造都面临理论障碍，需要新的设计思路。

Abstract: Generative AI disrupts the practice of giving credit to work that came before. Ideally, a generative model would give credit to any work on which its output depends in a significant way. \emph{Counterfactual credit attribution} (CCA) is a technical condition formalizing this goal--a relaxation of differential privacy--recently introduced by Livni, Moran, Nissim, and Pabbaraju [2024] who studied it in the PAC learning setting.
  We initiate the study of CCA generative models. Specifically, we consider autoregressive models giving credit to a deployment-time dataset (e.g., a RAG database). We uncover barriers to two natural approaches to CCA autoregressive models. First, we show that imposing CCA on the underlying next-token predictor does not guarantee that the model is CCA: CCA does not compose autoregressively (unlike DP). Second, we consider a different approach to building CCA models which we call \emph{retrofitting}. Retrofitting takes a model that does not attribute credit, and adds credit onto it. We prove a lower bound for CCA retrofitting under a weak optimality requirement. Given black-box access to the starting model, retrofitting requires query complexity exponential in the length of the model's outputs.

</details>


### [256] [Evaluating LLMs on Large-Scale Graph Property Estimation via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01484)
*Sunil Kumar Maurya,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EstGraph基准数据集，包含四个基于随机游走采样的大图属性估计任务，用于评估LLM在超大图（百万节点级）且图信息不完全可访问场景下的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理基准仅使用小图，受限于LLM上下文长度，无法评估LLM在真实世界中大规模且不可完全访问的图上的推理能力。

Method: 构建EstGraph基准，设计四个大图属性估计任务；采用随机游走采样方法从大图中提取子图信息，在上下文长度限制内为LLM提供有效信息；设计任务特定的提示构建策略。

Result: 在多种图数据集上评估了LLM在这四个估计任务上的推理能力，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM可以通过基于随机游走采样的提示构建方式，在有限上下文内对百万级节点的大图进行属性估计推理。

Abstract: With the rapidly improving reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is also a rising demand to use them in a wide variety of domains. This brings about the need to carefully evaluate the limits of the capabilities of these models with various tests and benchmarks. Graph structures are ubiquitous in real-world data, and are often used to represent and analyze relationship patterns within data. Many benchmarks have already been proposed in the graph literature to test the reasoning ability of LLMs to follow and execute graph algorithms. However, due to the limited context length of LLMs, these benchmarks consist of very small graphs. In real-world data, the size of graphs can be significantly larger, and in many cases, not fully accessible. In this paper, we examine a class of problems that arises with very large graphs having limited accessibility. We propose a large graph benchmark dataset, EstGraph, and introduce four distinct tasks designed to estimate large graph properties. We evaluate the reasoning abilities of LLMs on these tasks using a wide variety of graph datasets. In addition, we provide task-specific prompt constructions based on random walk sampling of large graphs (up to millions of nodes) that effectively convey sufficient information to LLMs within the limits of context length.

</details>


### [257] [Protein-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Full-Length mRNA Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01513)
*Zixi Shao,Tao Wang,Yibei Xiao,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMORNA是一个多目标生成框架，能够从目标蛋白序列直接从头设计完整的mRNA转录本。该框架先使用BART编码器-解码器模型在600多万天然蛋白-mRNA对上训练，再通过多目标组相对策略优化（MO-GRPO）同时优化稳定性、翻译效率和免疫安全性。以萤火虫荧光素酶为案例的评估表明，ProMORNA在预测的半衰期和翻译效率方面改进了帕累托前沿，并在预测功能得分上超越了基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计治疗性mRNA需要构建完整的转录本，并在这类转录本中平衡稳定性、翻译效率和免疫安全性，这是一项具有挑战性的多目标优化问题。现有方法难以同时兼顾这些相互冲突的目标。

Method: 首先使用BART风格的编码器-解码器架构在超过600万天然蛋白-mRNA对上进行监督预训练，然后引入MO-GRPO（多目标组相对策略优化）算法，以统一方式同时优化多个生物目标。

Result: 在萤火虫荧光素酶目标上的实验表明，ProMORNA在预测的半衰期和翻译效率上改进了帕累托前沿，并且在预测功能得分上超越了最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 多目标强化学习可用于在未见过的目标上进行全长度mRNA设计，在计算层面上验证了这一方法的可行性。

Abstract: Designing therapeutic messenger RNA (mRNA) requires creating full-length transcripts that carefully balance stability, translation efficiency, and immune safety. To address this challenge, we propose ProMORNA, a multi-objective generation framework that produces complete mRNA transcripts \textit{de novo} directly from a target protein sequence. Our approach begins by training a BART-style encoder-decoder model on over 6 million natural protein-mRNA pairs. We then introduce Multi-Objective Group Relative Policy Optimization (MO-GRPO) to simultaneously optimize for various biological objectives in a unified way. As a case study, we evaluated ProMORNA on the widely used firefly luciferase target, excluding it from both our supervised training data and the prompt pool. The results indicate that ProMORNA improves the \textit{in silico} Pareto frontier for predicted half-life and translation efficiency relative to standard supervised baselines. Additionally, it achieves higher predicted functional scores than a state-of-the-art baseline under the same evaluation pipeline. These computational findings demonstrate the feasibility of using multi-objective reinforcement learning for full-length mRNA design on unseen targets.

</details>


### [258] [ECG-biometrics-bench: A Unified Framework for Reproducible Benchmarking of ECG Biometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01548)
*Milad Parvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECG-biometrics-bench基准框架，系统揭示了ECG生物特征识别中因数据泄露（如会话内随机划分）导致的性能高估问题，并通过跨数据集、跨协议实验证明当前监督学习方法普遍存在时间漂移和未知身份导致的性能退化，提出动态多会话模板融合策略可部分缓解时间老化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG生物特征识别研究因数据泄露（如同会话内随机划分）导致结果过于乐观，缺乏标准化、可复现的评估基准来准确衡量真实性能。

Method: 构建模块化、可复现的ECG-biometrics-bench基准框架，统一7个公开ECG数据集的预处理、分割和评估流程；支持闭集和开集评估，以及跨会话、长期时间分离等渐进式真实协议；评估DeepECG、ResNet1D、CNN-LSTM等多种架构；提出动态多会话模板融合策略。

Result: 暴露了"随机划分谬误"：会话内评估协议人为膨胀性能，掩盖时间漂移和未见身份导致的严重退化；该退化具有模型无关性，是当前监督特征学习范式的固有问题；动态多会话模板融合可部分缓解时间老化导致性能退化。

Conclusion: 建立了ECG生物特征识别更真实的基线，指出了面向实际部署必须解决的关键挑战（时间漂移、未知身份泛化）。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) biometrics have emerged as a promising modality for continuous, liveness-aware authentication in wearable systems. However, many prior studies report overly optimistic results due to data leakage (e.g., random splits within the same session). To address this issue, we introduce ECG-biometrics-bench, a modular, reproducible benchmarking framework that standardizes preprocessing, segmentation, and evaluation across seven widely used public ECG datasets spanning clinical, ambulatory, and large-scale cohort settings. The framework supports both closed-set and open-set (i.e., subject-disjoint generalization in this work) evaluation, as well as progressively realistic protocols including cross-session and long-term temporal separation. To facilitate reproducible research in the community, the ECG-biometrics-bench repository will be made publicly accessible on GitHub upon the acceptance of this manuscript. Through a comprehensive multi-dataset analysis, we expose the Random Split Fallacy, demonstrating that intra-session evaluation protocols artificially inflate performance while masking severe degradation caused by temporal drift and unseen identities. Furthermore, by evaluating multiple architectures, including DeepECG, ResNet1D, and CNN-LSTM, we show that these failures are not model-specific but are likely inherent to current supervised feature-learning paradigms. Finally, we demonstrate that performance degradation due to temporal aging can be partially mitigated through a heavy enrollment, lightweight authentication strategy based on dynamic multi-session template fusion. These findings establish a more realistic baseline for ECG biometrics and highlight critical challenges that must be addressed for reliable real-world deployment.

</details>


### [259] [Importance-Guided Basis Selection for Low-Rank Decomposition of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01627)
*Daniel Agyei Asante,Ernie Chang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BSI是一种基于损失景观局部几何信息（二阶泰勒展开）的低秩分解框架，通过估计移除每个基对任务损失的预期增量来排序和剪枝基，并在数学推理任务上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩压缩方法（如Basel）基于重学习的奇异值系数大小进行剪枝，这种启发式方法忽略了损失景观的局部几何结构，导致剪枝决策与任务性能不一致。

Method: 通过奇异值上的二阶泰勒展开导出基于导数的重要性分数，结合一阶敏感性和二阶曲率量化剪枝影响；并开发了基于Hutchinson随机化探测方法的高效Hessian对角估计器，适配对称参数扰动下的损失曲率。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，BSI在低秩压缩中持续优于最先进基线，尤其在深度压缩场景下提升显著。

Conclusion: BSI提供了理论上有保证的损失增长上界、Hessian估计误差传播分析、方差刻画及高概率样本复杂度保证，是一种原则性的低秩压缩方法。

Abstract: Low-rank decomposition is a compelling approach for compressing large language models, but its effectiveness hinges on selecting which singular-vector bases to retain for a target task. Existing methods such as Basel adapt singular-value coefficients on downstream data and prune bases with small re-learned magnitudes, a heuristic that can be misaligned with task performance because it ignores the local geometry of the loss landscape. We present Basis Selection with Importance (BSI), a principled low-rank compression framework that ranks and prunes bases by directly estimating the expected loss increase incurred when each basis is removed. BSI derives a derivative-based importance score from a second-order Taylor expansion of the task loss with respect to singular values, combining first-order sensitivity and second-order curvature to quantify pruning impact. To make this criterion practical for LLMs, we develop an efficient Hessian-diagonal estimator by adapting the Hutchinson randomized-probing method to loss curvature with symmetric parameter perturbations. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis, including loss-increase bounds under basis pruning, explicit propagation of Hessian-diagonal estimation error into these bounds, variance characterization tied to the Hessian spectrum, high-probability sample-complexity guarantees for achieving a target estimation accuracy, and guidance on perturbation intensity. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that BSI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art low-rank decomposition baselines, with especially strong improvements under deep compression.

</details>


### [260] [Prescriptive Scaling Laws for Data Constrained Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01640)
*Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Srivatsa Kundurthy,Shriya Sudhakar,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据受限场景下重复训练导致过拟合的问题，提出一个带过拟合惩罚项的缩放律模型，并发现该模型可准确描述重复数据下的模型行为，给出了计算最优分配的新建议：当重复超过一定限度时，应将算力用于增加模型容量而非重复训练。此外，单参数形式可跨配置比较过拟合程度，案例表明强权重衰减可将过拟合系数降低约70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练算力增速超过高质量数据供给，传统Chinchilla缩放律假设每个token唯一，无法指导数据受限场景下的预训练决策，因此需要建立能刻画数据重复下过拟合效应的新缩放律。

Method: 在标准缩放律基础上引入一个简单的加性过拟合惩罚项来建模重复数据带来的额外损失，形成单参数形式的缩放律模型，并通过实验验证其预测准确性。

Result: 1）所提带过拟合惩罚的缩放律能准确描述重复数据下的模型行为；2）给出了数据受限下的计算最优分配建议：超出一定重复次数后应增加模型容量而非继续重复；3）强权重衰减（λ=1.0）可将过拟合系数降低约70%。

Conclusion: 在数据受限条件下，传统缩放律不再适用，需考虑重复数据带来的过拟合效应；所提方法不仅指导了计算最优分配，还通过单参数形式提供了跨配置比较过拟合程度的统一框架，并解释了为何数据受限时最优权重衰减需要显著增大。

Abstract: Training compute is increasingly outpacing the availability of high-quality data. This shifts the central challenge from optimal compute allocation to extracting maximum value from limited data. The widely adopted Chinchilla scaling law assumes every training token is unique. This limits its ability to guide pretraining decisions in data-constrained regimes. We model the excess loss under repetition with a simple additive overfitting penalty and find that it accurately describes model behavior. Our scaling law yields qualitatively new compute-optimal allocation advice. Beyond a point, further repetition is counterproductive and compute is better spent on model capacity. We show that following our law's recommended configuration improves performance in data-constrained regimes. Finally, because our one-parameter form isolates overfitting in a single coefficient, it enables direct comparison across training configurations. As a case study, we show that strong weight decay ($λ=1.0$) reduces this coefficient by approximately 70%, providing a scaling-law explanation for recent findings that optimal weight decay in data-constrained regimes is an order of magnitude larger than standard practice.

</details>


### [261] [AI Alignment via Incentives and Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01643)
*Rohit Agarwal,Joshua Lin,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过法律经济学中的威慑与执法模型，研究AI对齐问题。将不当行为视为对激励的理性反应，而非外部失败。提出对齐是固定点问题：更强的惩罚会抑制求解器行为，但也可能降低审计员的检查动力。论文形式化了一个双智能体模型，奖励设计被看作双层优化问题，并通过bandit外循环搜索奖励配置。实验表明，自适应奖励配置相比静态奖励能维持有效的监督压力，显著减少幻觉性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法通常将模型的不当行为视为外部失败（如训练不足或能力缺陷），忽视了在智能体系统中，求解器可能策略性地利用激励结构来获益（如给出有说服力但错误的答案、隐藏不确定性、利用虚假捷径）。作者认为需要从经济学的激励视角重新审视对齐问题。

Method: 1）构建求解器-审计员双智能体模型，将交互形式化为一个博弈：求解器选择行为（诚实/欺骗），审计员决定是否检查，奖励由联合校正结果决定。2）将奖励设计建模为双层优化问题——主智能体（委托人）通过调整奖励信号诱导均衡行为。3）提出基于bandit的外循环搜索算法，利用带噪交互反馈优化奖励配置。4）在LLM编码流水线上进行实验验证。

Result: 自适应奖励配置相比静态手工设计的奖励，能够维持有效的监督压力，改善主智能体对齐目标，并显著减少幻觉性错误尝试。

Conclusion: AI对齐不应仅关注最终答案的奖励，而应设计覆盖完整校正事件（错误发生、审计检查、错误被发现、监督激励是否持续）的奖励信号。奖励设计的有效性由其诱发的行为均衡决定，而非其语义含义。将法律经济学框架引入AI对齐，为理解智能体系统中的激励问题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We study AI alignment through the lens of law-and-economics models of deterrence and enforcement. In these models, misconduct is not treated as an external failure, but as a strategic response to incentives: an actor weighs the gain from violation against the probability of detection and the severity of punishment. We argue that the same logic arises naturally in agentic AI pipelines. A solver may benefit from producing a persuasive but incorrect answer, hiding uncertainty, or exploiting spurious shortcuts, while an auditor or verifier must decide whether costly monitoring is worthwhile.
